    56	# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    57	# Date Helpers (§14.7)
    58	# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    59	
    60	def get_today(demo_today: str | None = None) -> date:
    61	    """Return the base date for stagnation and schedule calculations.
    62	
    63	    Uses DEMO_TODAY environment variable (or argument) if set, otherwise UTC date.
    64	    """
    65	    raw = demo_today or os.environ.get("DEMO_TODAY")
    66	    if raw:
    67	        try:
    68	            # Handle ISO date (YYYY-MM-DD) or timestamp
    69	            if "T" in raw:
    70	                return datetime.fromisoformat(raw.replace("Z", "+00:00")).date()
    71	            return date.fromisoformat(raw.strip())
    72	        except (ValueError, TypeError):
    73	            pass
    74	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).date()
    75	
    76	
    77	def _parse_date_or_timestamp(val: str | None) -> date | None:
    78	    """Safely parse a date or ISO timestamp string into a date object."""
    79	    if not val or not str(val).strip():
    80	        return None
    81	    val_str = str(val).strip()
    82	    try:
    83	        if "T" in val_str:
    84	            return datetime.fromisoformat(val_str.replace("Z", "+00:00")).date()
    85	        return date.fromisoformat(val_str)
    86	    except (ValueError, TypeError):
    87	        return None
    88	
    89	
    90	# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    91	# Stagnation Score Calculation (§14.3)
    92	# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    93	
    94	def calculate_stagnation_score(
    95	    task: Task,
    96	    all_owner_tasks: list[Task],
    97	    today: date | None = None,
    98	    w_overdue: float = W_OVERDUE,
    99	    w_stale: float = W_STALE,
   100	    w_resched: float = W_RESCHED,
   101	    w_neglect: float = W_NEGLECT,
   102	    w_untouched: float = W_UNTOUCHED,
   103	    cap: int = STAGNATION_CAP,
   104	    neglect_window: int = STAGNATION_NEGLECT_WINDOW,
   105	) -> tuple[float, str, dict[str, Any]]:
   106	    """Compute rule-based stagnation score and synthesized evidence line.
   107	
   108	    Signals:
   109	    1. Overdue days: days past due date (capped).
   110	    2. Stale days: days since last update (capped).
   111	    3. Reschedule count: count of reschedules.
   112	    4. Relative neglect: task stale >= neglect_window while another task of owner updated within neglect_window.
   113	    5. Untouched: status == 'todo' and created_at == status_changed_at.
   114	
   115	    Returns:
   116	        (score, evidence_line, signals_dict)
   117	    """
   118	    ref_today = today or get_today()
   119	
   120	    # 1. Overdue days
   121	    due_d = _parse_date_or_timestamp(task.due_date)
   122	    overdue_days = max(0, (ref_today - due_d).days) if due_d is not None else 0
   123	    capped_overdue = min(overdue_days, cap)
   124	
   125	    # 2. Stale days
   126	    upd_d = _parse_date_or_timestamp(task.last_updated_at) or _parse_date_or_timestamp(task.created_at)
   127	    stale_days = max(0, (ref_today - upd_d).days) if upd_d is not None else 0
   128	    capped_stale = min(stale_days, cap)
   129	
   130	    # 3. Reschedule count
   131	    resched_count = max(0, task.reschedule_count)
   132	
   133	    # 4. Relative neglect
   134	    relative_neglect = 0
   135	    if stale_days >= neglect_window:
   136	        for other in all_owner_tasks:
   137	            if other.task_id == task.task_id:
   138	                continue
   139	            other_upd_d = _parse_date_or_timestamp(other.last_updated_at) or _parse_date_or_timestamp(other.created_at)
   140	            if other_upd_d is not None:
   141	                days_since_other = max(0, (ref_today - other_upd_d).days)
   142	                if days_since_other < neglect_window:
   143	                    relative_neglect = 1
   144	                    break
   145	
   146	    # 5. Untouched
   147	    untouched = 1 if (task.status == "todo" and task.status_changed_at == task.created_at) else 0
   148	
   149	    score = (
   150	        w_overdue * capped_overdue
   151	        + w_stale * capped_stale
   152	        + w_resched * resched_count
   153	        + w_neglect * relative_neglect
   154	        + w_untouched * untouched
   155	    )
   156	
   157	    # Synthesize evidence line deterministically (NO LLM, §14.3)
   158	    evidence_parts: list[str] = []
   159	    if resched_count > 0:
   160	        evidence_parts.append(f"Rescheduled {resched_count} time{'s' if resched_count > 1 else ''}")
   161	    if overdue_days > 0:
   162	        evidence_parts.append(f"overdue by {overdue_days} day{'s' if overdue_days > 1 else ''}")
   163	    if stale_days > 0:
   164	        evidence_parts.append(f"no updates for {stale_days} day{'s' if stale_days > 1 else ''}")
   165	    if relative_neglect == 1:
   166	        evidence_parts.append("inactive while other tasks are progressing")
   167	    if untouched == 1:
   168	        evidence_parts.append("not yet started")
   169	
   170	    if evidence_parts:
   171	        evidence_line = ", ".join(evidence_parts) + "."
   172	        evidence_line = evidence_line[0].upper() + evidence_line[1:]
   173	    else:
   174	        evidence_line = "No active stagnation signals."
   175	
   176	    signals = {
   177	        "overdue_days": overdue_days,
   178	        "capped_overdue": capped_overdue,
   179	        "stale_days": stale_days,
   180	        "capped_stale": capped_stale,
   181	        "reschedule_count": resched_count,
   182	        "relative_neglect": relative_neglect,
   183	        "untouched": untouched,
   184	        "score": score,
   185	    }
   186	
   187	    return score, evidence_line, signals
   188	
   189	
   190	# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   191	# Question Draft & Profile Diff Helpers
   192	# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   193	
   194	def generate_question_draft(task: Task, llm_client: Any | None = None) -> str:
   195	    """Generate an AI inquiry draft from task title and description (§14.4)."""
   196	    # Deterministic fallback template
   197	    desc_snippet = f": {task.description.strip()}" if task.description.strip() else ""
   198	    fallback = f"Seeking expertise and advice regarding {task.title}{desc_snippet}."
   199	
   200	    if llm_client is None:
   201	        return fallback
   202	
   203	    try:
   204	        model_name = os.environ.get("GEMINI_MODEL", "gemini-3.7-flash")
   205	        prompt = (
   206	            f"You are an AI assistant helping a colleague formulate a concise discovery inquiry.\n"
   207	            f"Task Title: {task.title}\n"
   208	            f"Task Description: {task.description}\n"
   209	            f"Write a single natural question (1-2 sentences in English) asking colleagues for advice or tacit knowledge to complete this task."
   210	        )
   211	        response = llm_client.models.generate_content(
   212	            model=model_name,
   213	            contents=prompt,
   214	        )
   215	        if hasattr(response, "text") and response.text:
   216	            cleaned = response.text.strip().strip('"').strip("'")
   217	            return cleaned if cleaned else fallback
   218	    except Exception:
   219	        pass
   220	
   221	    return fallback
   222	
   223	
   224	def extract_profile_diff(
   225	    mail: MailSeed,
   226	    current_profile: Profile | None = None,
   227	    llm_client: Any | None = None,
   228	) -> tuple[str, str] | None:
   229	    """Extract candidate profile item (item_key, body_draft) from unprocessed email (§14.5)."""
   230	    if not mail.body.strip() and not mail.subject.strip():
   231	        return None
   232	
   233	    if llm_client is not None:
   234	        try:
   235	            model_name = os.environ.get("GEMINI_MODEL", "gemini-3.7-flash")
   236	            existing_text = current_profile.get_full_text() if current_profile else ""
   237	            prompt = f"""You are analyzing a work email to see if it reveals tacit skills, current projects, or specialized background to add to the employee's professional profile.
   238	
   239	Current Profile:
   240	{existing_text}
   241	
   242	Email Subject: {mail.subject}
   243	Email Body: {mail.body}
   244	
   245	If this email contains meaningful professional experience or project context not yet in the profile, extract a suggested profile item in JSON format:
   246	{{
   247	  "item_key": "current_work" | "expertise" | "background",
   248	  "body_draft": "1-2 sentence description in English"
   249	}}
   250	If no meaningful knowledge is found, return null."""
   251	            response = llm_client.models.generate_content(
   252	                model=model_name,
   253	                contents=prompt,
   254	            )
   255	            resp_text = response.text.strip() if hasattr(response, "text") and response.text else ""
   256	            cleaned = re.sub(r"^```(?:json)?\s*", "", resp_text)
   257	            cleaned = re.sub(r"\s*```$", "", cleaned).strip()
   258	            if cleaned and cleaned != "null":
   259	                data = json.loads(cleaned)
   260	                if isinstance(data, dict) and "body_draft" in data:
   261	                    key = data.get("item_key", "current_work")
   262	                    body = data.get("body_draft", "").strip()
   263	                    if body:
   264	                        return key, body
   265	        except Exception:
   266	            pass
   267	
   268	    # Heuristic extraction fallback
   269	    subj = mail.subject.strip()
   270	    body = mail.body.strip()
   271	    if len(body) > 10:
   272	        return "current_work", f"{subj}: {body}"
   273	    return None
   274	
   275	
   276	# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   277	# Secretary Service Coordinator
   278	# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   279	
   280	class SecretaryService:
   281	    """Orchestrates secretary background sweep, morning digest, card CAS, and diff reviews."""
   282	
   283	    def __init__(
   284	        self,
   285	        store: Store,
   286	        kd_service: KnowledgeDiscoveryService,
   287	        matching_engine: MatchingEngine | None = None,
   288	        llm_client: Any | None = None,
   289	        t1: float = STAGNATION_T1,
   290	        t2: float = STAGNATION_T2,
   291	    ) -> None:
   292	        self.store = store
   293	        self.kd_service = kd_service
   294	        self.matching_engine = matching_engine or kd_service.matching_engine
   295	        self.llm_client = llm_client
   296	        self.t1 = t1
   297	        self.t2 = t2
   298	        assert self.t1 < self.t2, f"t1 ({self.t1}) must be < t2 ({self.t2})"
   299	
   300	    def run_sweep(self, demo_today: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
   301	        """Execute full secretary sweep across all tasks and mail seeds (§14.1, §14.2, §14.5).
   302	
   303	        State Machine Rules (§14.2):
   304	        1. Confirmed or Dismissed tasks: Do NOT recreate cards.
   305	        2. Task status 'done' or score < T1: Transition open card to 'resolved'.
   306	        3. T1 <= score < T2: Create or update 'notice' card.
   307	        4. score >= T2: Run preview search (public only, embedding_public). If candidates found,
   308	           create or promote card to 'request_draft'. If 0 candidates, keep/create 'notice'.
   309	        5. Idempotent: Never creates duplicate open cards for the same (owner, task_id).
   310	        """
   311	        ref_today = get_today(demo_today)
   312	        all_tasks = self.store.list_tasks()
   313	        registered_agents = self.store.list_agents(active_only=True)
   314	        profiles_list = self.store.list_profiles()
   315	        profiles_map = {p.employee_id: p for p in profiles_list}
   316	
   317	        # Group tasks by owner_employee_id
   318	        tasks_by_owner: dict[str, list[Task]] = {}
   319	        for t in all_tasks:
   320	            tasks_by_owner.setdefault(t.owner_employee_id, []).append(t)
   321	
   322	        tasks_evaluated = 0
   323	        cards_created = 0
   324	        cards_promoted = 0
   325	        cards_updated = 0
   326	        cards_resolved = 0
   327	        diff_cards_created = 0
   328	
   329	        for owner_id, owner_tasks in tasks_by_owner.items():
   330	            # Resolve sender entity for audit recording
   331	            owner_agent = self.store.get_agent_by_employee_id(owner_id)
   332	            sender_entity = owner_agent.agent_id if owner_agent else owner_id
   333	
   334	            for task in owner_tasks:
   335	                tasks_evaluated += 1
   336	
   337	                # Rule 1: Skip if already confirmed or dismissed
   338	                existing_cards = self.store.find_cards_for_task(owner_id, task.task_id)
   339	                has_terminal = any(c.status in ("confirmed", "dismissed") for c in existing_cards)
   340	                if has_terminal:
   341	                    continue
   342	
   343	                open_card = self.store.find_open_card_for_task(owner_id, task.task_id)
   344	
   345	                # Rule 2: If task is done -> resolve open card
   346	                if task.status == "done":
   347	                    if open_card is not None:
   348	                        open_card.status = "resolved"
   349	                        open_card.resolved_reason = "task_done"
   350	                        open_card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   351	                        self.store.save_card(open_card)
   352	                        cards_resolved += 1
   353	                    continue
   354	
   355	                # Calculate score
   356	                score, evidence_line, _ = calculate_stagnation_score(
   357	                    task, owner_tasks, today=ref_today
   358	                )
   359	
   360	                # Rule 2b: If score < T1 -> resolve open card
   361	                if score < self.t1:
   362	                    if open_card is not None:
   363	                        open_card.status = "resolved"
   364	                        open_card.resolved_reason = "score_below_t1"
   365	                        open_card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   366	                        self.store.save_card(open_card)
   367	                        cards_resolved += 1
   368	                    continue
   369	
   370	                # Rule 3: T1 <= score < T2 (Notice Tier)
   371	                if self.t1 <= score < self.t2:
   372	                    if open_card is None:
   373	                        card_id = f"card_stag_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:10]}"
   374	                        new_card = Card(
   375	                            card_id=card_id,
   376	                            owner_employee_id=owner_id,
   377	                            type="stagnation",
   378	                            tier="notice",
   379	                            payload={
   380	                                "task_id": task.task_id,
   381	                                "task_title": task.title,
   382	                                "score": score,
   383	                                "evidence_line": evidence_line,
   384	                            },
   385	                            status="open",
   386	                        )
   387	                        self.store.save_card(new_card)
   388	                        cards_created += 1
   389	
   390	                        # Audit log (§14.6)
   391	                        self.kd_service.transmission.send(
   392	                            from_entity=sender_entity,
   393	                            to_entity="system",
   394	                            intent="stagnation_detected",
   395	                            payload_type="stagnation_detected",
   396	                            payload={"task_id": task.task_id, "tier": "notice", "score": score},
   397	                        )
   398	                    else:
   399	                        # Update existing open card without downgrading tier
   400	                        open_card.payload["score"] = score
   401	                        open_card.payload["evidence_line"] = evidence_line
   402	                        open_card.payload["task_title"] = task.title
   403	                        open_card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   404	                        self.store.save_card(open_card)
   405	                        cards_updated += 1
   406	                    continue
   407	
   408	                # Rule 4: score >= T2 (Request Draft Tier or Notice fallback)
   409	                if score >= self.t2:
   410	                    # 1. Draft inquiry question
   411	                    q_draft = generate_question_draft(task, llm_client=self.llm_client)
   412	
   413	                    # 2. Pure preview search on embedding_public
   414	                    preview_cands = self.matching_engine.preview_search(
   415	                        question=q_draft,
   416	                        registered_agents=registered_agents,
   417	                        profiles=profiles_map,
   418	                        exclude_employee_id=owner_id,
   419	                    )
   420	
   421	                    if len(preview_cands) == 0:
   422	                        tier = "notice"
   423	                        preview_dict: dict[str, Any] = {
   424	                            "candidates": [],
   425	                            "note": "No matching candidates found across public profiles.",
   426	                        }
   427	                    else:
   428	                        tier = "request_draft"
   429	                        preview_dict = {
   430	                            "candidates": [
   431	                                {
   432	                                    "employee_id": c.employee_id,
   433	                                    "name": c.name,
   434	                                    "reason_text": c.reason_text,
   435	                                    "score": c.score,
   436	                                    "cited_item_keys": c.cited_item_keys,
   437	                                }
   438	                                for c in preview_cands
   439	                            ]
   440	                        }
   441	
   442	                    if open_card is None:
   443	                        card_id = f"card_stag_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:10]}"
   444	                        new_card = Card(
   445	                            card_id=card_id,
   446	                            owner_employee_id=owner_id,
   447	                            type="stagnation",
   448	                            tier=tier,
   449	                            payload={
   450	                                "task_id": task.task_id,
   451	                                "task_title": task.title,
   452	                                "score": score,
   453	                                "evidence_line": evidence_line,
   454	                                "question_draft": q_draft,
   455	                                "preview": preview_dict,
   456	                            },
   457	                            status="open",
   458	                        )
   459	                        self.store.save_card(new_card)
   460	                        cards_created += 1
   461	
   462	                        # Audit log: stagnation_detected
   463	                        self.kd_service.transmission.send(
   464	                            from_entity=sender_entity,
   465	                            to_entity="system",
   466	                            intent="stagnation_detected",
   467	                            payload_type="stagnation_detected",
   468	                            payload={"task_id": task.task_id, "tier": tier, "score": score},
   469	                        )
   470	
   471	                        # Audit log: preview_search if candidates found
   472	                        if preview_cands:
   473	                            self.kd_service.transmission.send(
   474	                                from_entity=sender_entity,
   475	                                to_entity="system",
   476	                                intent="preview_search",
   477	                                payload_type="preview_search",
   478	                                payload={
   479	                                    "task_id": task.task_id,
   480	                                    "candidates": [
   481	                                        {"employee_id": c.employee_id, "score": c.score}
   482	                                        for c in preview_cands
   483	                                    ],
   484	                                },
   485	                            )
   486	                    else:
   487	                        # Existing card: promote notice -> request_draft or update
   488	                        was_notice = (open_card.tier == "notice")
   489	                        open_card.tier = tier
   490	                        open_card.payload["score"] = score
   491	                        open_card.payload["evidence_line"] = evidence_line
   492	                        open_card.payload["task_title"] = task.title
   493	                        open_card.payload["question_draft"] = q_draft
   494	                        open_card.payload["preview"] = preview_dict
   495	                        open_card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   496	                        self.store.save_card(open_card)
   497	
   498	                        if was_notice and tier == "request_draft":
   499	                            cards_promoted += 1
   500	                        else:
   501	                            cards_updated += 1
   502	
   503	                        # Record preview_search audit if preview executed
   504	                        if preview_cands:
   505	                            self.kd_service.transmission.send(
   506	                                from_entity=sender_entity,
   507	                                to_entity="system",
   508	                                intent="preview_search",
   509	                                payload_type="preview_search",
   510	                                payload={
   511	                                    "task_id": task.task_id,
   512	                                    "candidates": [
   513	                                        {"employee_id": c.employee_id, "score": c.score}
   514	                                        for c in preview_cands
   515	                                    ],
   516	                                },
   517	                            )
   518	
   519	        # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   520	        # Sweep Mail Seeds for Profile Diff Proposals (§14.5)
   521	        # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   522	        unprocessed_mails = self.store.list_mail_seeds(unprocessed_only=True)
   523	        for mail in unprocessed_mails:
   524	            owner_prof = self.store.get_profile(mail.owner_employee_id)
   525	            diff_res = extract_profile_diff(mail, owner_prof, llm_client=self.llm_client)
   526	
   527	            if diff_res is not None:
   528	                item_key, body_draft = diff_res
   529	                card_id = f"card_diff_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:10]}"
   530	                diff_card = Card(
   531	                    card_id=card_id,
   532	                    owner_employee_id=mail.owner_employee_id,
   533	                    type="profile_diff",
   534	                    tier=None,
   535	                    payload={
   536	                        "item_key": item_key,
   537	                        "body_draft": body_draft,
   538	                        "source_mail_id": mail.mail_id,
   539	                        "subject": mail.subject,
   540	                    },
   541	                    status="open",
   542	                )
   543	                self.store.save_card(diff_card)
   544	                diff_cards_created += 1
   545	
   546	                owner_agent = self.store.get_agent_by_employee_id(mail.owner_employee_id)
   547	                sender_entity = owner_agent.agent_id if owner_agent else mail.owner_employee_id
   548	                self.kd_service.transmission.send(
   549	                    from_entity=sender_entity,
   550	                    to_entity="system",
   551	                    intent="profile_diff_proposed",
   552	                    payload_type="profile_diff_proposed",
   553	                    payload={"mail_id": mail.mail_id, "item_key": item_key},
   554	                )
   555	
   556	            # Mark processed
   557	            mail.processed = True
   558	            self.store.save_mail_seed(mail)
   559	
   560	        return {
   561	            "status": "ok",
   562	            "date": ref_today.isoformat(),
   563	            "tasks_evaluated": tasks_evaluated,
   564	            "cards_created": cards_created,
   565	            "cards_promoted": cards_promoted,
   566	            "cards_updated": cards_updated,
   567	            "cards_resolved": cards_resolved,
   568	            "diff_cards_created": diff_cards_created,
   569	        }
   570	
   571	    def get_morning_digest(
   572	        self,
   573	        employee_id: str,
   574	        demo_today: str | None = None,
   575	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   576	        """Assemble dynamic morning digest for employee (§14.2, §14.8).
   577	
   578	        Schedule rule order: Overdue -> Today -> Tomorrow -> Upcoming.
   579	        Followed by open stagnation and profile_diff cards.
   580	        """
   581	        ref_today = get_today(demo_today)
   582	        all_schedules = self.store.list_schedules(owner_employee_id=employee_id)
   583	
   584	        # Categorize and order schedule reminders
   585	        reminders: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   586	        for s in all_schedules:
   587	            due_d = _parse_date_or_timestamp(s.due_date)
   588	            if due_d is None:
   589	                category = "upcoming"
   590	                priority = 4
   591	            elif due_d < ref_today:
   592	                category = "overdue"
   593	                priority = 1
   594	            elif due_d == ref_today:
   595	                category = "today"
   596	                priority = 2
   597	            elif (due_d - ref_today).days == 1:
   598	                category = "tomorrow"
   599	                priority = 3
   600	            else:
   601	                category = "upcoming"
   602	                priority = 4
   603	
   604	            reminders.append({
   605	                "item_id": s.item_id,
   606	                "kind": s.kind,
   607	                "title": s.title,
   608	                "due_date": s.due_date,
   609	                "due_category": category,
   610	                "_priority": priority,
   611	            })
   612	
   613	        # Sort: priority ascending, then due_date ascending
   614	        reminders.sort(key=lambda r: (r["_priority"], r["due_date"]))
   615	        for r in reminders:
   616	            r.pop("_priority", None)
   617	
   618	        # Retrieve open cards
   619	        open_cards = self.store.list_cards(owner_employee_id=employee_id, status="open")
   620	        stagnation_cards = [
   621	            c.to_dict() for c in open_cards if c.type == "stagnation"
   622	        ]
   623	        profile_diff_cards = [
   624	            c.to_dict() for c in open_cards if c.type == "profile_diff"
   625	        ]
   626	
   627	        return {
   628	            "employee_id": employee_id,
   629	            "date": ref_today.isoformat(),
   630	            "reminders": reminders,
   631	            "stagnation_cards": stagnation_cards,
   632	            "profile_diff_cards": profile_diff_cards,
   633	        }
   634	
   635	    def confirm_stagnation_card(
   636	        self,
   637	        card_id: str,
   638	        edited_question: str,
   639	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   640	        """Confirm stagnation card and dispatch query via standard query path (§14.4).
   641	
   642	        Idempotency & CAS:
   643	        - If already 'confirmed', returns existing linked_query_audit_id without resubmitting.
   644	        - If not 'open', raises an error.
   645	        - On query execution failure, rolls back card status to 'open'.
   646	        """
   647	        card = self.store.get_card(card_id)
   648	        if card is None:
   649	            raise ValueError(f"Secretary card '{card_id}' not found")
   650	
   651	        # Idempotent double-POST / double-click check
   652	        if card.status == "confirmed":
   653	            return {
   654	                "status": "already_confirmed",
   655	                "card_id": card.card_id,
   656	                "query_audit_id": card.linked_query_audit_id,
   657	            }
   658	
   659	        if card.status != "open":
   660	            raise ValueError(f"Card '{card_id}' cannot be confirmed in status '{card.status}'")
   661	
   662	        # Atomic CAS: mark confirmed
   663	        card.status = "confirmed"
   664	        self.store.save_card(card)
   665	
   666	        try:
   667	            # Pass edited question to existing query pipeline
   668	            result = self.kd_service.submit_query(
   669	                requester_id=card.owner_employee_id,
   670	                question_text=edited_question,
   671	            )
   672	            query_audit_id = result.query_message.audit_id
   673	            card.linked_query_audit_id = query_audit_id
   674	            card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   675	            self.store.save_card(card)
   676	
   677	            return {
   678	                "status": "confirmed",
   679	                "card_id": card.card_id,
   680	                "query_audit_id": query_audit_id,
   681	                "dispatched_count": len(result.dispatched_asks),
   682	            }
   683	        except Exception as exc:
   684	            # Rollback on submission failure
   685	            card.status = "open"
   686	            card.linked_query_audit_id = None
   687	            card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   688	            self.store.save_card(card)
   689	            raise RuntimeError(f"Failed to submit discovery query: {exc}") from exc
   690	
   691	    def review_profile_diff(
   692	        self,
   693	        card_id: str,
   694	        action: str,  # 'apply' | 'edit_apply' | 'private_apply' | 'dismiss'
   695	        edited_body: str | None = None,
   696	        item_key: str | None = None,
   697	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   698	        """Process employee review for a profile diff proposal card (§14.5).
   699	
   700	        Actions:
   701	        - 'apply': Add item with visibility='public', regenerate embeddings, card -> 'applied'.
   702	        - 'edit_apply': Add edited body with visibility='public', regenerate embeddings, card -> 'applied'.
   703	        - 'private_apply': Add item with visibility='private', regenerate embeddings, card -> 'applied'.
   704	        - 'dismiss': Card -> 'dismissed' (no profile change).
   705	        """
   706	        card = self.store.get_card(card_id)
   707	        if card is None or card.type != "profile_diff":
   708	            raise ValueError(f"Profile diff card '{card_id}' not found")
   709	        if card.status != "open":
   710	            raise ValueError(f"Card '{card_id}' is already {card.status}")
   711	
   712	        if action == "dismiss":
   713	            card.status = "dismissed"
   714	            card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   715	            self.store.save_card(card)
   716	            return {"status": "dismissed", "card_id": card_id}
   717	
   718	        if action not in ("apply", "edit_apply", "private_apply"):
   719	            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported review action '{action}'")
   720	
   721	        key = item_key or card.payload.get("item_key", "current_work")
   722	        body = (
   723	            edited_body.strip()
   724	            if (action == "edit_apply" and edited_body and edited_body.strip())
   725	            else str(card.payload.get("body_draft", "")).strip()
   726	        )
   727	        visibility = "private" if action == "private_apply" else "public"
   728	
   729	        # Update profile
   730	        prof = self.store.get_profile(card.owner_employee_id)
   731	        if prof is None:
   732	            prof = Profile(
   733	                employee_id=card.owner_employee_id,
   734	                name=card.owner_employee_id,
   735	                role="Employee",
   736	                items=[],
   737	            )
   738	
   739	        # Check if item with same key already exists
   740	        existing_item = prof.get_item(key)
   741	        if existing_item is not None:
   742	            existing_item.body = body
   743	            existing_item.source = "mail_seed"
   744	            existing_item.visibility = visibility
   745	            existing_item.reviewed = True
   746	        else:
   747	            new_item = ProfileItem(
   748	                key=key,
   749	                body=body,
   750	                source="mail_seed",
   751	                visibility=visibility,
   752	                reviewed=True,
   753	            )
   754	            prof.items.append(new_item)
   755	
   756	        # Regenerate BOTH full embedding and public embedding (§14.5, §9.3)
   757	        self.matching_engine.compute_profile_embedding(prof)
   758	        self.store.save_profile(prof)
   759	
   760	        # Update card status
   761	        card.status = "applied"
   762	        card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   763	        self.store.save_card(card)
   764	
   765	        return {
   766	            "status": "applied",
   767	            "card_id": card_id,
   768	            "item_key": key,
   769	            "visibility": visibility,
   770	            "reviewed": True,
   771	        }
   772	
   773	    def dismiss_card(self, card_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
   774	        """Dismiss a secretary card (§14.2)."""
   775	        card = self.store.get_card(card_id)
   776	        if card is None:
   777	            raise ValueError(f"Card '{card_id}' not found")
   778	        card.status = "dismissed"
   779	        card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   780	        self.store.save_card(card)
   781	        return {"status": "dismissed", "card_id": card_id}

   307	@dataclass
   308	class RequesterCandidateStatus:
   309	    """Candidate status as viewed from requester UI.
   310	
   311	    Never reveals whether connection was based on private profile items.
   312	    Status state values:
   313	    - 'pending': 返答待ち
   314	    - 'matched': つながりました（MTG提案あり）
   315	    - 'declined': 今回は難しいそうです（理由+添付表示）

   325	
   326	@dataclass
   327	class Task:
   328	    """Task entity for secretary stagnation detection (§14.2).
   329	
   330	    Attributes:
   331	        task_id: Unique task identifier.
   332	        owner_employee_id: Identifier of the employee owning this task.
   333	        title: Task title.
   334	        description: Detailed task description.
   335	        status: 'todo' | 'in_progress' | 'done'.
   336	        due_date: Due date in ISO format (YYYY-MM-DD).
   337	        created_at: ISO8601 creation timestamp.
   338	        last_updated_at: ISO8601 last updated timestamp.
   339	        reschedule_count: Number of times this task has been rescheduled.
   340	        status_changed_at: ISO8601 timestamp of last status change.
   341	    """
   342	
   343	    task_id: str
   344	    owner_employee_id: str
   345	    title: str
   346	    description: str = ""
   347	    status: str = "todo"  # "todo" | "in_progress" | "done"
   348	    due_date: str = ""
   349	    created_at: str = field(default_factory=utc_now_iso)
   350	    last_updated_at: str = field(default_factory=utc_now_iso)
   351	    reschedule_count: int = 0
   352	    status_changed_at: str = field(default_factory=utc_now_iso)
   353	
   354	    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   355	        return {
   356	            "task_id": self.task_id,
   357	            "owner_employee_id": self.owner_employee_id,
   358	            "title": self.title,
   359	            "description": self.description,
   360	            "status": self.status,
   361	            "due_date": self.due_date,
   362	            "created_at": self.created_at,
   363	            "last_updated_at": self.last_updated_at,
   364	            "reschedule_count": self.reschedule_count,
   365	            "status_changed_at": self.status_changed_at,
   366	        }
   367	
   368	    @classmethod
   369	    def from_dict(cls, data: dict[str, Any]) -> Task:
   370	        return cls(
   371	            task_id=data["task_id"],
   372	            owner_employee_id=data["owner_employee_id"],
   373	            title=data.get("title", ""),
   374	            description=data.get("description", ""),
   375	            status=data.get("status", "todo"),
   376	            due_date=data.get("due_date", ""),
   377	            created_at=data.get("created_at", utc_now_iso()),
   378	            last_updated_at=data.get("last_updated_at", utc_now_iso()),
   379	            reschedule_count=int(data.get("reschedule_count", 0)),
   380	            status_changed_at=data.get("status_changed_at", utc_now_iso()),
   381	        )
   382	
   383	
   384	@dataclass
   385	class Schedule:
   386	    """Schedule reminder entity for morning digest (§14.2).
   387	
   388	    Attributes:
   389	        item_id: Unique schedule identifier.
   390	        owner_employee_id: Identifier of the employee.
   391	        kind: 'expense_deadline' | 'weekly_report' | 'monthly_report' |
   392	              'meeting_prep' | 'meeting_review' | 'journal'.
   393	        title: Schedule title / description.
   394	        due_date: Due date in ISO format (YYYY-MM-DD).
   395	    """
   396	
   397	    item_id: str
   398	    owner_employee_id: str
   399	    kind: str
   400	    title: str
   401	    due_date: str
   402	
   403	    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   404	        return {
   405	            "item_id": self.item_id,
   406	            "owner_employee_id": self.owner_employee_id,
   407	            "kind": self.kind,
   408	            "title": self.title,
   409	            "due_date": self.due_date,
   410	        }
   411	
   412	    @classmethod
   413	    def from_dict(cls, data: dict[str, Any]) -> Schedule:
   414	        return cls(
   415	            item_id=data["item_id"],
   416	            owner_employee_id=data["owner_employee_id"],
   417	            kind=data.get("kind", ""),
   418	            title=data.get("title", ""),
   419	            due_date=data.get("due_date", ""),
   420	        )
   421	
   422	
   423	@dataclass
   424	class MailSeed:
   425	    """Email seed record for proactive profile diff proposals (§14.2, §14.5).
   426	
   427	    Attributes:
   428	        mail_id: Unique mail identifier.
   429	        owner_employee_id: Employee owning the mailbox.
   430	        subject: Mail subject line.
   431	        body: Mail body text.
   432	        received_at: ISO8601 received timestamp.
   433	        processed: Whether a profile diff card has been generated.
   434	    """
   435	
   436	    mail_id: str
   437	    owner_employee_id: str
   438	    subject: str
   439	    body: str
   440	    received_at: str = field(default_factory=utc_now_iso)
   441	    processed: bool = False
   442	
   443	    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   444	        return {
   445	            "mail_id": self.mail_id,
   446	            "owner_employee_id": self.owner_employee_id,
   447	            "subject": self.subject,
   448	            "body": self.body,
   449	            "received_at": self.received_at,
   450	            "processed": self.processed,
   451	        }
   452	
   453	    @classmethod
   454	    def from_dict(cls, data: dict[str, Any]) -> MailSeed:
   455	        return cls(
   456	            mail_id=data["mail_id"],
   457	            owner_employee_id=data["owner_employee_id"],
   458	            subject=data.get("subject", ""),
   459	            body=data.get("body", ""),
   460	            received_at=data.get("received_at", utc_now_iso()),
   461	            processed=bool(data.get("processed", False)),
   462	        )
   463	
   464	
   465	@dataclass
   466	class Card:
   467	    """Secretary card for stagnation alerts or profile diff proposals (§14.2).
   468	
   469	    Attributes:
   470	        card_id: Unique card identifier.
   471	        owner_employee_id: Identifier of the card recipient employee.
   472	        type: 'stagnation' | 'profile_diff'.
   473	        tier: 'notice' | 'request_draft' | None.
   474	        payload: Card payload (stagnation details or profile diff proposal).
   475	        status: 'open' | 'confirmed' | 'dismissed' | 'applied' | 'resolved'.
   476	        resolved_reason: 'task_done' | 'score_below_t1' | None.
   477	        linked_query_audit_id: Linked query audit_id if confirmed.
   478	        created_at: ISO8601 timestamp.
   479	        updated_at: ISO8601 timestamp.
   480	    """
   481	
   482	    card_id: str
   483	    owner_employee_id: str
   484	    type: str  # "stagnation" | "profile_diff"
   485	    tier: str | None = None  # "notice" | "request_draft" | None
   486	    payload: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
   487	    status: str = "open"  # "open" | "confirmed" | "dismissed" | "applied" | "resolved"
   488	    resolved_reason: str | None = None  # "task_done" | "score_below_t1" | None
   489	    linked_query_audit_id: str | None = None
   490	    created_at: str = field(default_factory=utc_now_iso)
   491	    updated_at: str = field(default_factory=utc_now_iso)
   492	
   493	    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   494	        return {
   495	            "card_id": self.card_id,
   496	            "owner_employee_id": self.owner_employee_id,
   497	            "type": self.type,
   498	            "tier": self.tier,
   499	            "payload": dict(self.payload),
   500	            "status": self.status,
   501	            "resolved_reason": self.resolved_reason,
   502	            "linked_query_audit_id": self.linked_query_audit_id,
   503	            "created_at": self.created_at,
   504	            "updated_at": self.updated_at,
   505	        }
   506	
   507	    @classmethod
   508	    def from_dict(cls, data: dict[str, Any]) -> Card:
   509	        return cls(
   510	            card_id=data["card_id"],
   511	            owner_employee_id=data["owner_employee_id"],
   512	            type=data.get("type", "stagnation"),
   513	            tier=data.get("tier"),
   514	            payload=dict(data.get("payload", {})),
   515	            status=data.get("status", "open"),
   516	            resolved_reason=data.get("resolved_reason"),
   517	            linked_query_audit_id=data.get("linked_query_audit_id"),
   518	            created_at=data.get("created_at", utc_now_iso()),
   519	            updated_at=data.get("updated_at", utc_now_iso()),
   520	        )
   521	
   522	
   523	@dataclass
   524	class PreviewCandidate:
   525	    """Candidate identified during secretary preview search (§14.4).
   526	
   527	    Strict requirement: Generated purely using public items & embedding_public.
   528	    """
   529	
   530	    employee_id: str
   531	    name: str
   532	    reason_text: str
   533	    cited_item_keys: list[str]
   534	    score: float
   535	
     1	"""Storage abstraction and in-memory implementation for knowledge discovery.
     2	
     3	Follows design.md §3:
     4	- Store interface defining contracts for agents, profiles, and messages.
     5	- InMemoryStore providing a standalone, testable implementation without Firestore dependency.
     6	"""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
    11	from copy import deepcopy
    12	from typing import Any
    13	
    14	from knowledge_discovery.models import (
    15	    Agent,
    16	    Card,
    17	    MailSeed,
    18	    Message,
    19	    Profile,
    20	    Schedule,
    21	    Task,
    22	)
    23	
    24	
    25	class Store(ABC):
    26	    """Abstract storage interface for knowledge discovery entities."""
    27	
    28	    # Agent operations (agents collection / registry)
    29	    @abstractmethod
    30	    def save_agent(self, agent: Agent) -> None:
    31	        """Save or update an agent record in the registry."""
    32	        pass
    33	
    34	    @abstractmethod
    35	    def get_agent(self, agent_id: str) -> Agent | None:
    36	        """Retrieve an agent by agent_id."""
    37	        pass
    38	
    39	    @abstractmethod
    40	    def get_agent_by_employee_id(self, employee_id: str) -> Agent | None:
    41	        """Retrieve an agent by employee_id."""
    42	        pass
    43	
    44	    @abstractmethod
    45	    def list_agents(self, active_only: bool = False) -> list[Agent]:
    46	        """List registered agents, optionally filtering for active only."""
    47	        pass
    48	
    49	    # Profile operations (profiles collection)
    50	    @abstractmethod
    51	    def save_profile(self, profile: Profile) -> None:
    52	        """Save or update an employee knowledge profile."""
    53	        pass
    54	
    55	    @abstractmethod
    56	    def get_profile(self, employee_id: str) -> Profile | None:
    57	        """Retrieve a profile by employee_id."""
    58	        pass
    59	
    60	    @abstractmethod
    61	    def list_profiles(self) -> list[Profile]:
    62	        """List all employee profiles."""
    63	        pass
    64	
    65	    # Message operations (messages collection / audit log)
    66	    @abstractmethod
    67	    def save_message(self, message: Message) -> None:
    68	        """Save or update a message record."""
    69	        pass
    70	
    71	    @abstractmethod
    72	    def get_message(self, audit_id: str) -> Message | None:
    73	        """Retrieve a message by audit_id."""
    74	        pass
    75	
    76	    @abstractmethod
    77	    def list_messages(self, limit: int | None = None) -> list[Message]:
    78	        """List all messages in chronological order."""
    79	        pass
    80	
    81	    @abstractmethod
    82	    def get_messages_for_entity(self, entity_id: str) -> list[Message]:
    83	        """Retrieve messages involving the given entity (as sender, recipient, or participant)."""
    84	        pass
    85	
    86	    # Task operations (tasks collection §14.2)
    87	    @abstractmethod
    88	    def save_task(self, task: Task) -> None:
    89	        """Save or update a task record."""
    90	        pass
    91	
    92	    @abstractmethod
    93	    def get_task(self, task_id: str) -> Task | None:
    94	        """Retrieve a task by task_id."""
    95	        pass
    96	
    97	    @abstractmethod
    98	    def list_tasks(self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None) -> list[Task]:
    99	        """List tasks, optionally filtered by owner_employee_id."""
   100	        pass
   101	
   102	    # Schedule operations (schedules collection §14.2)
   103	    @abstractmethod
   104	    def save_schedule(self, schedule: Schedule) -> None:
   105	        """Save or update a schedule reminder."""
   106	        pass
   107	
   108	    @abstractmethod
   109	    def get_schedule(self, item_id: str) -> Schedule | None:
   110	        """Retrieve a schedule by item_id."""
   111	        pass
   112	
   113	    @abstractmethod
   114	    def list_schedules(self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None) -> list[Schedule]:
   115	        """List schedules, optionally filtered by owner_employee_id."""
   116	        pass
   117	
   118	    # MailSeed operations (mail_seeds collection §14.2, §14.5)
   119	    @abstractmethod
   120	    def save_mail_seed(self, mail: MailSeed) -> None:
   121	        """Save or update an email seed record."""
   122	        pass
   123	
   124	    @abstractmethod
   125	    def get_mail_seed(self, mail_id: str) -> MailSeed | None:
   126	        """Retrieve an email seed by mail_id."""
   127	        pass
   128	
   129	    @abstractmethod
   130	    def list_mail_seeds(
   131	        self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None, unprocessed_only: bool = False
   132	    ) -> list[MailSeed]:
   133	        """List mail seeds, optionally filtered by owner and/or unprocessed status."""
   134	        pass
   135	
   136	    # Card operations (cards collection §14.2)
   137	    @abstractmethod
   138	    def save_card(self, card: Card) -> None:
   139	        """Save or update a secretary card."""
   140	        pass
   141	
   142	    @abstractmethod
   143	    def get_card(self, card_id: str) -> Card | None:
   144	        """Retrieve a card by card_id."""
   145	        pass
   146	
   147	    @abstractmethod
   148	    def list_cards(
   149	        self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None, status: str | None = None
   150	    ) -> list[Card]:
   151	        """List cards, optionally filtered by owner_employee_id and/or status."""
   152	        pass
   153	
   154	    @abstractmethod
   155	    def find_open_card_for_task(self, owner_employee_id: str, task_id: str) -> Card | None:
   156	        """Find an open stagnation card for the given task and owner."""
   157	        pass
   158	
   159	    @abstractmethod
   160	    def find_cards_for_task(self, owner_employee_id: str, task_id: str) -> list[Card]:
   161	        """Find all stagnation cards for the given task and owner."""
   162	        pass
   163	
   164	    @abstractmethod
   165	    def clear(self) -> None:
   166	        """Clear all stored data (useful for test isolation)."""
   167	        pass
   168	
   169	
   170	class InMemoryStore(Store):
   171	    """In-memory implementation of Store using Python data structures."""
   172	
   173	    def __init__(self) -> None:
   174	        self._agents: dict[str, Agent] = {}
   175	        self._profiles: dict[str, Profile] = {}
   176	        self._messages: list[Message] = []
   177	        self._messages_by_id: dict[str, Message] = {}
   178	        self._tasks: dict[str, Task] = {}
   179	        self._schedules: dict[str, Schedule] = {}
   180	        self._mail_seeds: dict[str, MailSeed] = {}
   181	        self._cards: dict[str, Card] = {}
   182	
   183	    def save_agent(self, agent: Agent) -> None:
   184	        """Save or update an agent record in the registry."""
   185	        stored = deepcopy(agent)
   186	        self._agents[stored.agent_id] = stored
   187	
   188	    def get_agent(self, agent_id: str) -> Agent | None:
   189	        """Retrieve an agent by agent_id."""
   190	        agent = self._agents.get(agent_id)
   191	        return deepcopy(agent) if agent is not None else None
   192	
   193	    def get_agent_by_employee_id(self, employee_id: str) -> Agent | None:
   194	        """Retrieve an agent by employee_id."""
   195	        for agent in self._agents.values():
   196	            if agent.employee_id == employee_id:
   197	                return deepcopy(agent)
   198	        return None
   199	
   200	    def list_agents(self, active_only: bool = False) -> list[Agent]:
   201	        """List registered agents, optionally filtering for active only."""
   202	        agents = list(self._agents.values())
   203	        if active_only:
   204	            agents = [a for a in agents if a.active]
   205	        return [deepcopy(a) for a in agents]
   206	
   207	    def save_profile(self, profile: Profile) -> None:
   208	        """Save or update an employee knowledge profile."""
   209	        stored = deepcopy(profile)
   210	        self._profiles[stored.employee_id] = stored
   211	
   212	    def get_profile(self, employee_id: str) -> Profile | None:
   213	        """Retrieve a profile by employee_id."""
   214	        profile = self._profiles.get(employee_id)
   215	        return deepcopy(profile) if profile is not None else None
   216	
   217	    def list_profiles(self) -> list[Profile]:
   218	        """List all employee profiles."""
   219	        return [deepcopy(p) for p in self._profiles.values()]
   220	
   221	    def save_message(self, message: Message) -> None:
   222	        """Save or update a message record."""
   223	        stored = deepcopy(message)
   224	        if stored.audit_id in self._messages_by_id:
   225	            for idx, existing in enumerate(self._messages):
   226	                if existing.audit_id == stored.audit_id:
   227	                    self._messages[idx] = stored
   228	                    break
   229	        else:
   230	            self._messages.append(stored)
   231	        self._messages_by_id[stored.audit_id] = stored
   232	
   233	    def get_message(self, audit_id: str) -> Message | None:
   234	        """Retrieve a message by audit_id."""
   235	        msg = self._messages_by_id.get(audit_id)
   236	        return deepcopy(msg) if msg is not None else None
   237	
   238	    def list_messages(self, limit: int | None = None) -> list[Message]:
   239	        """List all messages in chronological order."""
   240	        messages = [deepcopy(m) for m in self._messages]
   241	        if limit is not None and limit > 0:
   242	            return messages[-limit:]
   243	        return messages
   244	
   245	    def get_messages_for_entity(self, entity_id: str) -> list[Message]:
   246	        """Retrieve messages involving the given entity (as sender, recipient, or participant)."""
   247	        results: list[Message] = []
   248	        for msg in self._messages:
   249	            participants = msg.payload.get("participants", []) if isinstance(msg.payload, dict) else []
   250	            if (
   251	                msg.from_entity == entity_id
   252	                or msg.to_entity == entity_id
   253	                or entity_id in participants
   254	            ):
   255	                results.append(deepcopy(msg))
   256	        return results
   257	
   258	    # Task operations
   259	    def save_task(self, task: Task) -> None:
   260	        stored = deepcopy(task)
   261	        self._tasks[stored.task_id] = stored
   262	
   263	    def get_task(self, task_id: str) -> Task | None:
   264	        task = self._tasks.get(task_id)
   265	        return deepcopy(task) if task is not None else None
   266	
   267	    def list_tasks(self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None) -> list[Task]:
   268	        tasks = list(self._tasks.values())
   269	        if owner_employee_id is not None:
   270	            tasks = [t for t in tasks if t.owner_employee_id == owner_employee_id]
   271	        return [deepcopy(t) for t in tasks]
   272	
   273	    # Schedule operations
   274	    def save_schedule(self, schedule: Schedule) -> None:
   275	        stored = deepcopy(schedule)
   276	        self._schedules[stored.item_id] = stored
   277	
   278	    def get_schedule(self, item_id: str) -> Schedule | None:
   279	        s = self._schedules.get(item_id)
   280	        return deepcopy(s) if s is not None else None
   281	
   282	    def list_schedules(self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None) -> list[Schedule]:
   283	        schedules = list(self._schedules.values())
   284	        if owner_employee_id is not None:
   285	            schedules = [s for s in schedules if s.owner_employee_id == owner_employee_id]
   286	        return [deepcopy(s) for s in schedules]
   287	
   288	    # MailSeed operations
   289	    def save_mail_seed(self, mail: MailSeed) -> None:
   290	        stored = deepcopy(mail)
   291	        self._mail_seeds[stored.mail_id] = stored
   292	
   293	    def get_mail_seed(self, mail_id: str) -> MailSeed | None:
   294	        m = self._mail_seeds.get(mail_id)
   295	        return deepcopy(m) if m is not None else None
   296	
   297	    def list_mail_seeds(
   298	        self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None, unprocessed_only: bool = False
   299	    ) -> list[MailSeed]:
   300	        mails = list(self._mail_seeds.values())
   301	        if owner_employee_id is not None:
   302	            mails = [m for m in mails if m.owner_employee_id == owner_employee_id]
   303	        if unprocessed_only:
   304	            mails = [m for m in mails if not m.processed]
   305	        return [deepcopy(m) for m in mails]
   306	
   307	    # Card operations
   308	    def save_card(self, card: Card) -> None:
   309	        stored = deepcopy(card)
   310	        self._cards[stored.card_id] = stored
   311	
   312	    def get_card(self, card_id: str) -> Card | None:
   313	        c = self._cards.get(card_id)
   314	        return deepcopy(c) if c is not None else None
   315	
   316	    def list_cards(
   317	        self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None, status: str | None = None
   318	    ) -> list[Card]:
   319	        cards = list(self._cards.values())
   320	        if owner_employee_id is not None:
   321	            cards = [c for c in cards if c.owner_employee_id == owner_employee_id]
   322	        if status is not None:
   323	            cards = [c for c in cards if c.status == status]
   324	        return [deepcopy(c) for c in cards]
   325	
   326	    def find_open_card_for_task(self, owner_employee_id: str, task_id: str) -> Card | None:
   327	        for c in self._cards.values():
   328	            if (
   329	                c.owner_employee_id == owner_employee_id
   330	                and c.type == "stagnation"
   331	                and c.status == "open"
   332	                and c.payload.get("task_id") == task_id
   333	            ):
   334	                return deepcopy(c)
   335	        return None
   336	
   337	    def find_cards_for_task(self, owner_employee_id: str, task_id: str) -> list[Card]:
   338	        results: list[Card] = []
   339	        for c in self._cards.values():
   340	            if (
   341	                c.owner_employee_id == owner_employee_id
   342	                and c.type == "stagnation"
   343	                and c.payload.get("task_id") == task_id
   344	            ):
   345	                results.append(deepcopy(c))
   346	        return results
   347	
   348	    def clear(self) -> None:
   349	        """Clear all stored data."""
   350	        self._agents.clear()
   351	        self._profiles.clear()
   352	        self._messages.clear()
   353	        self._messages_by_id.clear()
   354	        self._tasks.clear()
   355	        self._schedules.clear()
   356	        self._mail_seeds.clear()
   357	        self._cards.clear()
     1	"""Firestore implementation of the Store interface for knowledge discovery.
     2	
     3	Follows design.md §3:
     4	- agents collection: agents/{agent_id}
     5	- profiles collection: profiles/{employee_id}
     6	- messages collection: messages/{audit_id}
     7	- In-memory vector similarity computation over fetched profiles (avoiding composite index validation uncertainties).
     8	"""
     9	
    10	from __future__ import annotations
    11	
    12	from typing import Any
    13	
    14	from knowledge_discovery.models import (
    15	    Agent,
    16	    Card,
    17	    MailSeed,
    18	    Message,
    19	    Profile,
    20	    Schedule,
    21	    Task,
    22	)
    23	from knowledge_discovery.store import Store
    24	
    25	try:
    26	    from google.cloud import firestore
    27	except ImportError:
    28	    firestore = None  # type: ignore[assignment]
    29	
    30	
    31	class FirestoreStore(Store):
    32	    """Google Cloud Firestore implementation of Store interface."""
    33	
    34	    def __init__(
    35	        self,
    36	        client: Any | None = None,
    37	        project: str | None = None,
    38	        database: str | None = None,
    39	    ) -> None:
    40	        """Initialize FirestoreStore with either an injected client or new Firestore Client."""
    41	        if client is not None:
    42	            self.db = client
    43	        elif firestore is not None:
    44	            self.db = firestore.Client(project=project, database=database)
    45	        else:
    46	            raise RuntimeError(
    47	                "google-cloud-firestore is not installed and no custom client was provided."
    48	            )
    49	
    50	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    51	    # Agent operations (agents collection / registry)
    52	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    53	
    54	    def save_agent(self, agent: Agent) -> None:
    55	        """Save or update an agent record in the agents collection."""
    56	        doc_ref = self.db.collection("agents").document(agent.agent_id)
    57	        doc_ref.set(agent.to_dict())
    58	
    59	    def get_agent(self, agent_id: str) -> Agent | None:
    60	        """Retrieve an agent by agent_id."""
    61	        doc = self.db.collection("agents").document(agent_id).get()
    62	        if hasattr(doc, "exists") and not doc.exists:
    63	            return None
    64	        data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None

   146	    def list_messages(self, limit: int | None = None) -> list[Message]:
   147	        """List all messages in chronological order."""
   148	        col = self.db.collection("messages")
   149	        # In Firestore, retrieve and sort in-memory or by timestamp
   150	        docs = col.stream()
   151	        messages: list[Message] = []
   152	        for doc in docs:
   153	            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   154	            if data:
   155	                messages.append(Message.from_dict(data))
   156	
   157	        # Sort chronologically by timestamp
   158	        messages.sort(key=lambda m: m.timestamp)
   159	
   160	        if limit is not None and limit > 0:
   161	            return messages[-limit:]
   162	        return messages
   163	
   164	    def get_messages_for_entity(self, entity_id: str) -> list[Message]:
   165	        """Retrieve messages involving the given entity (sender, recipient, or participant)."""
   166	        all_msgs = self.list_messages()
   167	        results: list[Message] = []
   168	        for msg in all_msgs:
   169	            participants = (
   170	                msg.payload.get("participants", []) if isinstance(msg.payload, dict) else []
   171	            )
   172	            if (
   173	                msg.from_entity == entity_id
   174	                or msg.to_entity == entity_id
   175	                or entity_id in participants
   176	            ):
   177	                results.append(msg)
   178	        return results

   323	        docs = (
   324	            col.where("owner_employee_id", "==", owner_employee_id)
   325	            .where("type", "==", "stagnation")

   327	            .where("payload.task_id", "==", task_id)
   328	            .limit(1)
   329	            .stream()
   330	        )

   332	            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   333	            if data:
   334	                return Card.from_dict(data)
   335	        return None

   342	            .where("type", "==", "stagnation")
   343	            .where("payload.task_id", "==", task_id)

   345	        )
   346	        cards: list[Card] = []
   347	        for doc in docs:
   348	            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   349	            if data:
   350	                cards.append(Card.from_dict(data))

   357	            docs = col.stream()

   162	    const API_KEY = (new URLSearchParams(location.search)).get("api_key") || "";
   163	    if (!API_KEY) {

   167	    function esc(v) {
   168	      return String(v ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;","'":"&#39;"}[c]));
   169	    }
   170	    const REQUESTER_ID = "emp_jordan_lee";
   171	    let pollInterval = null;

   174	      1: "A hospital client of mine wants to relocate one of their clinics. Who in our group knows how to find sites that can actually host a medical facility — zoning, conversion, that kind of thing?",
   175	      2: "The owner of a small clinic I work with mentioned she's thinking about retiring in a few years. Who has experience with practice succession conversations?",
   176	      3: "We need someone with experience in ambulatory surgical center operational licensing and state compliance documentation."
   177	    };

   192	    async function fetchDigest() {
   193	      try {
   194	        const res = await fetch(`/api/secretary/digest?employee_id=${REQUESTER_ID}`, {
   195	          headers: { "X-API-Key": API_KEY }
   196	        });
   197	        if (!res.ok) return;
   198	        const data = await res.json();
   199	        renderDigest(data);
   200	      } catch (err) {
   201	        console.error("Digest fetch error:", err);
   202	      }
   203	    }

   205	    async function triggerSweep() {
   206	      try {
   207	        const res = await fetch("/api/secretary/sweep", {
   208	          method: "POST",
   209	          headers: {
   210	            "Content-Type": "application/json",
   211	            "X-API-Key": API_KEY,
   212	          },
   213	          body: JSON.stringify({})
   214	        });
   215	        if (!res.ok) {
   216	          alert("Sweep failed: " + res.statusText);
   217	          return;
   218	        }
   219	        await fetchDigest();
   220	      } catch (err) {
   221	        alert("Sweep error: " + err.message);
   222	      }
   223	    }

   225	    function renderDigest(data) {
   226	      document.getElementById("digestDateBadge").innerText = `Date: ${data.date || "Today"}`;
   227	      
   228	      // 1. Render Reminders (Overdue -> Today -> Tomorrow -> Upcoming)
   229	      const reminderList = document.getElementById("reminderList");
   230	      const reminders = data.reminders || [];
   231	      if (reminders.length === 0) {
   232	        reminderList.innerHTML = '<div style="font-size:12px; color:var(--text-muted); padding:6px;">No pending schedule deadlines for today.</div>';
   233	      } else {
   234	        reminderList.innerHTML = reminders.map(r => {
   235	          let tagClass = "upcoming";
   236	          let tagText = r.due_date;
   237	          if (r.due_category === "overdue") { tagClass = "overdue"; tagText = "Overdue"; }
   238	          else if (r.due_category === "today") { tagClass = "today"; tagText = "Due Today"; }
   239	          else if (r.due_category === "tomorrow") { tagClass = "tomorrow"; tagText = "Due Tomorrow"; }
   240	
   241	          return `
   242	            <div class="reminder-row">
   243	              <div><strong>${esc(r.title)}</strong></div>
   244	              <span class="status-tag ${tagClass}">${tagText} (${esc(r.due_date)})</span>
   245	            </div>`;
   246	        }).join("");
   247	      }
   248	
   249	      // 2. Render Stagnation Cards & Profile Diff Cards
   250	      const cardsContainer = document.getElementById("digestCardsContainer");
   251	      const stagCards = data.stagnation_cards || [];
   252	      const diffCards = data.profile_diff_cards || [];
   253	
   254	      let cardsHtml = "";
   255	
   256	      // Stagnation cards
   257	      stagCards.forEach(card => {
   258	        const p = card.payload || {};
   259	        const isDraft = (card.tier === "request_draft");
   260	        const cands = (p.preview && p.preview.candidates) ? p.preview.candidates : [];
   261	
   262	        let candsHtml = "";
   263	        if (isDraft && cands.length > 0) {
   264	          candsHtml = `
   265	            <div style="font-size:12px; color:#93c5fd; font-weight:600; margin-top:8px;">Suggested Candidates (Public Profile Match):</div>
   266	            <div class="cand-preview-box">
   267	              ${cands.map(c => `<div>• <strong>${esc(c.name || c.employee_id)}</strong>: <em>"${esc(c.reason_text)}"</em></div>`).join("")}
   268	            </div>`;
   269	        }
   270	
   271	        cardsHtml += `
   272	          <div class="secretary-card-box" id="card_box_${esc(card.card_id)}">
   273	            <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:flex-start;">
   274	              <div>
   275	                <span class="evidence-bar">⚠️ ${esc(p.evidence_line || "Stagnation detected")}</span>
   276	                <h3 style="font-size:14px; font-weight:600; color:#e2e8f0; margin-bottom:4px;">Task: ${esc(p.task_title || p.task_id)}</h3>
   277	              </div>
   278	              <span class="badge" style="background:#1e293b; color:#93c5fd;">${isDraft ? "Request Draft Ready" : "Notice"}</span>
   279	            </div>
   280	
   281	            ${candsHtml}
   282	
   283	            ${isDraft ? `
   284	              <div style="margin-top:10px;">
   285	                <label style="color:#cbd5e1; font-size:12px; font-weight:600;">🤖 AI Inquiry Draft (Review & Edit):</label>
   286	                <textarea id="draft_${esc(card.card_id)}" style="height:65px; margin-bottom:6px;">${esc(p.question_draft || "")}</textarea>
   287	                <div class="fixed-notice">Candidates may differ at request time — the full search runs only after you confirm.</div>
   288	                <div class="btn-group">
   289	                  <button onclick="confirmStagnationCard('${esc(card.card_id)}')">Request an intro</button>
   290	                  <button class="secondary" onclick="dismissCard('${esc(card.card_id)}')">Dismiss</button>
   291	                </div>
   292	              </div>
   293	            ` : `
   294	              <div style="margin-top:8px;">
   295	                <div style="font-size:12px; color:var(--text-muted); margin-bottom:8px;">This task is experiencing stagnation. Consider reviewing or reaching out to colleagues.</div>
   296	                <button class="secondary" onclick="dismissCard('${esc(card.card_id)}')">Dismiss</button>
   297	              </div>
   298	            `}
   299	          </div>`;
   300	      });
   301	
   302	      // Profile diff cards (§14.5)
   303	      diffCards.forEach(card => {
   304	        const p = card.payload || {};
   305	        cardsHtml += `
   306	          <div class="diff-box" id="card_box_${esc(card.card_id)}">
   307	            <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center;">
   308	              <span style="font-size:13px; font-weight:600; color:#6ee7b7;">📄 Profile Update Suggestion</span>
   309	              <span class="badge" style="background:#064e3b; color:#a7f3d0;">From recent email</span>
   310	            </div>
   311	            <div style="font-size:12px; color:#cbd5e1; margin-top:6px;">
   312	              <strong>Subject:</strong> ${esc(p.subject || "Email")}
   313	            </div>
   314	            <div class="diff-preview">[${esc(p.item_key || "current_work")}]: ${esc(p.body_draft || "")}</div>
   315	            <div id="diff_edit_box_${esc(card.card_id)}" style="display:none; margin-bottom:10px;">
   316	              <textarea id="diff_edit_text_${esc(card.card_id)}" style="height:60px;">${esc(p.body_draft || "")}</textarea>
   317	              <button onclick="submitEditDiff('${esc(card.card_id)}')">Save & Apply</button>
   318	            </div>
   319	            <div class="btn-group" style="margin-top:6px;">
   320	              <button onclick="reviewDiff('${esc(card.card_id)}', 'apply')">Apply (Public)</button>
   321	              <button class="secondary" onclick="toggleEditDiff('${esc(card.card_id)}')">Edit & Apply</button>
   322	              <button class="secondary" onclick="reviewDiff('${esc(card.card_id)}', 'private_apply')">Apply as Private</button>
   323	              <button class="danger-btn" onclick="reviewDiff('${esc(card.card_id)}', 'dismiss')">Dismiss</button>
   324	            </div>
   325	          </div>`;
   326	      });
   327	
   328	      cardsContainer.innerHTML = cardsHtml;
   329	    }

   331	    async function confirmStagnationCard(cardId) {
   332	      const textarea = document.getElementById(`draft_${cardId}`);
   333	      const editedQuestion = textarea ? textarea.value.trim() : "";
   334	      if (!editedQuestion) {
   335	        alert("Please enter a question to dispatch.");
   336	        return;
   337	      }
   338	
   339	      try {
   340	        const res = await fetch("/api/secretary/confirm", {
   341	          method: "POST",
   342	          headers: {
   343	            "Content-Type": "application/json",
   344	            "X-API-Key": API_KEY,
   345	          },
   346	          body: JSON.stringify({
   347	            card_id: cardId,
   348	            edited_question: editedQuestion
   349	          })
   350	        });
   351	        const data = await res.json();
   352	        if (!res.ok) {
   353	          alert("Confirm failed: " + (data.detail || "Error"));
   354	          return;
   355	        }
   356	
   357	        document.getElementById("queryMeta").innerText = 
   358	          `Confirmed Inquiry ID: ${data.query_audit_id} | Dispatched via Secretary Confirm`;
   359	        document.getElementById("liveStatus").style.display = "inline-flex";
   360	
   361	        await fetchDigest();
   362	        await fetchStatuses();
   363	
   364	        if (pollInterval) clearInterval(pollInterval);
   365	        pollInterval = setInterval(fetchStatuses, 2000);
   366	      } catch (err) {
   367	        alert("Confirm error: " + err.message);
   368	      }
   369	    }
   370	
   371	    async function dismissCard(cardId) {
   372	      try {
   373	        const res = await fetch(`/api/secretary/cards/${encodeURIComponent(cardId)}/dismiss`, {
   374	          method: "POST",
   375	          headers: { "X-API-Key": API_KEY }
   376	        });
   377	        if (!res.ok) {
   378	          alert("Dismiss failed");
   379	          return;
   380	        }
   381	        await fetchDigest();
   382	      } catch (err) {
   383	        alert("Dismiss error: " + err.message);
   384	      }
   385	    }
   386	
   387	    function toggleEditDiff(cardId) {
   388	      const box = document.getElementById(`diff_edit_box_${cardId}`);
   389	      if (box) {
   390	        box.style.display = (box.style.display === "none") ? "block" : "none";
   391	      }
   392	    }
   393	
   394	    async function submitEditDiff(cardId) {
   395	      const textEl = document.getElementById(`diff_edit_text_${cardId}`);
   396	      const edited = textEl ? textEl.value.trim() : "";
   397	      await reviewDiff(cardId, "edit_apply", edited);
   398	    }
   399	
   400	    async function reviewDiff(cardId, action, editedBody = null) {
   401	      try {
   402	        const res = await fetch(`/api/secretary/profile-diff/${encodeURIComponent(cardId)}/review`, {
   403	          method: "POST",
   404	          headers: {
   405	            "Content-Type": "application/json",
   406	            "X-API-Key": API_KEY,
   407	          },
   408	          body: JSON.stringify({
   409	            action: action,
   410	            edited_body: editedBody
   411	          })
   412	        });
   413	        const data = await res.json();
   414	        if (!res.ok) {
   415	          alert("Review failed: " + (data.detail || "Error"));
   416	          return;
   417	        }
   418	        await fetchDigest();
   419	      } catch (err) {
   420	        alert("Review error: " + err.message);
   421	      }
   422	    }
