   424	    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   425	    // Query & Requester Statuses
   426	    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   427	
   428	    async function submitInquiry() {
   429	      const q = document.getElementById("questionText").value.trim();
   430	      if (!q) {
   431	        alert("Please enter a question.");
   432	        return;
   433	      }
   434	      const submitBtn = document.getElementById("submitBtn");
   435	      submitBtn.disabled = true;
   436	      submitBtn.innerText = "Dispatching...";
   437	
   438	      try {
   439	        const res = await fetch("/api/query", {
   440	          method: "POST",
   441	          headers: {
   442	            "Content-Type": "application/json",
   443	            "X-API-Key": API_KEY,
   444	          },
   445	          body: JSON.stringify({
   446	            requester_id: REQUESTER_ID,
   447	            question_text: q
   448	          })
   449	        });
   450	        const data = await res.json();
   451	        if (!res.ok) {
   452	          alert("Error dispatching query: " + (data.detail || "Unknown error"));
   453	          return;
   454	        }
   455	
   456	        document.getElementById("queryMeta").innerText = 
   457	          `Inquiry ID: ${data.query_id} | Dispatched to ${data.dispatched_count} candidate agent(s) | Top 20 screened in Screen Funnel`;
   458	        
   459	        document.getElementById("liveStatus").style.display = "inline-flex";
   460	        await fetchStatuses();
   461	
   462	        if (pollInterval) clearInterval(pollInterval);
   463	        pollInterval = setInterval(fetchStatuses, 2000);
   464	      } catch (err) {
   465	        alert("Failed to connect to server: " + err.message);
   466	      } finally {
   467	        submitBtn.disabled = false;
   468	        submitBtn.innerText = "Dispatch Inquiry";
   469	      }
   470	    }
   471	
   472	    async function fetchStatuses() {
   473	      try {
   474	        const res = await fetch(`/api/requester/${REQUESTER_ID}/status`, {
   475	          headers: { "X-API-Key": API_KEY }
   476	        });
   477	        if (!res.ok) return;
   478	        const data = await res.json();
   479	        renderStatuses(data.statuses || []);
   480	      } catch (err) {
   481	        console.error("Status fetch error:", err);
   482	      }
   483	    }
   484	
   485	    function renderStatuses(statuses) {
   486	      const container = document.getElementById("statusContainer");
   487	      if (!statuses || statuses.length === 0) {
   488	        container.innerHTML = `
   489	          <div style="font-size: 13px; color: var(--text-muted); text-align: center; padding: 24px;">
   490	            No candidate responses recorded yet.
   491	          </div>`;
   492	        return;
   493	      }
   494	
   495	      container.innerHTML = statuses.map((item, idx) => {
   496	        if (item.state === "pending") {
   497	          return `
   498	            <div class="status-card pending">
   499	              <div class="status-header">
   500	                <span class="status-title">Candidate #${idx + 1}</span>
   501	                <span class="status-tag pending">Waiting for response</span>
   502	              </div>
   503	              <div class="status-body">
   504	                Candidate's personal agent is evaluating tacit background fit. Awaiting candidate review and consent.
   505	              </div>
   506	            </div>`;
   507	        } else if (item.state === "matched") {
   508	          return `
   509	            <div class="status-card matched">
   510	              <div class="status-header">
   511	                <span class="status-title">Connected with ${esc(item.respondent_name) || "Candidate"}</span>
   512	                <span class="status-tag matched">Connected</span>
   513	              </div>
   514	              <div class="status-body">
   515	                <strong>Synergy Match Established:</strong> ${esc(item.respondent_name)} consented to connect!
   516	                <br>Proposed: <strong>15-minute introductory meeting</strong>.
   517	              </div>
   518	            </div>`;
   519	        } else if (item.state === "declined") {
   520	          let attHtml = "";
   521	          if (item.attachment) {
   522	            if (item.attachment.type === "link") {
   523	              attHtml = `<div class="attachment-box">📎 <strong>Shared Link:</strong> <a href="${/^https?:\/\//.test(item.attachment.content) ? esc(item.attachment.content) : "#"}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${esc(item.attachment.content)}</a></div>`;
   524	            } else if (item.attachment.type === "doc") {
   525	              attHtml = `<div class="attachment-box">📄 <strong>Shared Document:</strong> <a href="/attachments/${encodeURIComponent(item.attachment.content)}?api_key=${encodeURIComponent(API_KEY)}" target="_blank">${esc(item.attachment.content)}</a></div>`;
   526	            } else {
   527	              attHtml = `<div class="attachment-box">📝 <strong>Note:</strong> ${esc(item.attachment.content)}</div>`;
   528	            }
   529	          }
   530	          return `
   531	            <div class="status-card declined">
   532	              <div class="status-header">
   533	                <span class="status-title">${esc(item.respondent_name) || "Candidate"}</span>
   534	                <span class="status-tag declined">Unavailable this time</span>
   535	              </div>
   536	              <div class="status-body">
   537	                ${item.decline_reason ? `<em>"${esc(item.decline_reason)}"</em>` : "Candidate cannot connect at this time."}
   538	                ${attHtml}
   539	              </div>
   540	            </div>`;
   541	        }
   542	        return "";
   543	      }).join("");
   544	    }
   545	
   546	    // Initial load
   547	    fetchDigest();
   548	    fetchStatuses();
   549	  </script>

   334	      if (!editedQuestion) {

   341	          method: "POST",
   342	          headers: {
   343	            "Content-Type": "application/json",
   344	            "X-API-Key": API_KEY,
   345	          },
   346	          body: JSON.stringify({
   347	            card_id: cardId,
   348	            edited_question: editedQuestion
   349	          })

   362	        await fetchStatuses();

   400	    async function reviewDiff(cardId, action, editedBody = null) {
   401	      try {
   402	        const res = await fetch(`/api/secretary/profile-diff/${encodeURIComponent(cardId)}/review`, {
   403	          method: "POST",
   404	          headers: {
   405	            "Content-Type": "application/json",
   406	            "X-API-Key": API_KEY,
   407	          },
   408	          body: JSON.stringify({
   409	            action: action,
   410	            edited_body: editedBody
   411	          })
   412	        });
   413	        const data = await res.json();
   414	        if (!res.ok) {
   415	          alert("Review failed: " + (data.detail || "Error"));
   416	          return;
   417	        }
   418	        await fetchDigest();
   419	      } catch (err) {
   420	        alert("Review error: " + err.message);
   421	      }
   422	    }

   327	        for c in self._cards.values():
   328	            if (
   329	                c.owner_employee_id == owner_employee_id
   330	                and c.type == "stagnation"
   331	                and c.status == "open"
   332	                and c.payload.get("task_id") == task_id

   184	    def save_task(self, task: Task) -> None:
   185	        """Save or update a task record."""
   186	        doc_ref = self.db.collection("tasks").document(task.task_id)
   187	        doc_ref.set(task.to_dict())
   188	
   189	    def get_task(self, task_id: str) -> Task | None:
   190	        """Retrieve a task by task_id."""
   191	        doc = self.db.collection("tasks").document(task_id).get()
   192	        if hasattr(doc, "exists") and not doc.exists:
   193	            return None
   194	        data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   195	        if not data:
   196	            return None
   197	        return Task.from_dict(data)
   198	
   199	    def list_tasks(self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None) -> list[Task]:
   200	        """List tasks, optionally filtered by owner_employee_id."""
   201	        col = self.db.collection("tasks")
   202	        if owner_employee_id is not None:
   203	            docs = col.where("owner_employee_id", "==", owner_employee_id).stream()
   204	        else:
   205	            docs = col.stream()
   206	        tasks: list[Task] = []
   207	        for doc in docs:
   208	            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   209	            if data:
   210	                tasks.append(Task.from_dict(data))
   211	        return tasks

   213	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   214	    # Schedule operations (schedules collection)
   215	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   216	
   217	    def save_schedule(self, schedule: Schedule) -> None:
   218	        """Save or update a schedule reminder."""
   219	        doc_ref = self.db.collection("schedules").document(schedule.item_id)
   220	        doc_ref.set(schedule.to_dict())
   221	
   222	    def get_schedule(self, item_id: str) -> Schedule | None:
   223	        """Retrieve a schedule by item_id."""
   224	        doc = self.db.collection("schedules").document(item_id).get()
   225	        if hasattr(doc, "exists") and not doc.exists:
   226	            return None
   227	        data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   228	        if not data:
   229	            return None
   230	        return Schedule.from_dict(data)
   231	
   232	    def list_schedules(self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None) -> list[Schedule]:
   233	        """List schedules, optionally filtered by owner_employee_id."""
   234	        col = self.db.collection("schedules")
   235	        if owner_employee_id is not None:
   236	            docs = col.where("owner_employee_id", "==", owner_employee_id).stream()
   237	        else:
   238	            docs = col.stream()
   239	        schedules: list[Schedule] = []
   240	        for doc in docs:
   241	            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   242	            if data:
   243	                schedules.append(Schedule.from_dict(data))
   244	        return schedules
   245	
   246	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   247	    # MailSeed operations (mail_seeds collection)
   248	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   249	
   250	    def save_mail_seed(self, mail: MailSeed) -> None:
   251	        """Save or update an email seed record."""
   252	        doc_ref = self.db.collection("mail_seeds").document(mail.mail_id)
   253	        doc_ref.set(mail.to_dict())
   254	
   255	    def get_mail_seed(self, mail_id: str) -> MailSeed | None:
   256	        """Retrieve an email seed by mail_id."""
   257	        doc = self.db.collection("mail_seeds").document(mail_id).get()
   258	        if hasattr(doc, "exists") and not doc.exists:
   259	            return None
   260	        data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   261	        if not data:
   262	            return None
   263	        return MailSeed.from_dict(data)
   264	
   265	    def list_mail_seeds(
   266	        self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None, unprocessed_only: bool = False
   267	    ) -> list[MailSeed]:
   268	        """List mail seeds, optionally filtered by owner and/or unprocessed status."""
   269	        col = self.db.collection("mail_seeds")
   270	        query = col
   271	        if owner_employee_id is not None:
   272	            query = query.where("owner_employee_id", "==", owner_employee_id)
   273	        if unprocessed_only:
   274	            query = query.where("processed", "==", False)
   275	        docs = query.stream()
   276	        mails: list[MailSeed] = []
   277	        for doc in docs:
   278	            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   279	            if data:
   280	                mails.append(MailSeed.from_dict(data))
   281	        return mails
   282	
   283	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   284	    # Card operations (cards collection)
   285	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   286	
   287	    def save_card(self, card: Card) -> None:
   288	        """Save or update a secretary card."""
   289	        doc_ref = self.db.collection("cards").document(card.card_id)
   290	        doc_ref.set(card.to_dict())
   291	
   292	    def get_card(self, card_id: str) -> Card | None:
   293	        """Retrieve a card by card_id."""
   294	        doc = self.db.collection("cards").document(card_id).get()
   295	        if hasattr(doc, "exists") and not doc.exists:
   296	            return None
   297	        data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   298	        if not data:
   299	            return None
   300	        return Card.from_dict(data)
   301	
   302	    def list_cards(
   303	        self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None, status: str | None = None
   304	    ) -> list[Card]:
   305	        """List cards, optionally filtered by owner_employee_id and/or status."""
   306	        col = self.db.collection("cards")
   307	        query = col
   308	        if owner_employee_id is not None:
   309	            query = query.where("owner_employee_id", "==", owner_employee_id)
   310	        if status is not None:
   311	            query = query.where("status", "==", status)
   312	        docs = query.stream()
   313	        cards: list[Card] = []
   314	        for doc in docs:
   315	            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   316	            if data:
   317	                cards.append(Card.from_dict(data))
   318	        return cards
   319	
   320	    def find_open_card_for_task(self, owner_employee_id: str, task_id: str) -> Card | None:
   321	        """Find an open stagnation card for the given task and owner."""
   322	        col = self.db.collection("cards")
   323	        docs = (
   324	            col.where("owner_employee_id", "==", owner_employee_id)
   325	            .where("type", "==", "stagnation")
   326	            .where("status", "==", "open")
   327	            .where("payload.task_id", "==", task_id)
   328	            .limit(1)
   329	            .stream()
   330	        )
   331	        for doc in docs:
   332	            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   333	            if data:
   334	                return Card.from_dict(data)
   335	        return None
   336	
   337	    def find_cards_for_task(self, owner_employee_id: str, task_id: str) -> list[Card]:
   338	        """Find all stagnation cards for the given task and owner."""
   339	        col = self.db.collection("cards")
   340	        docs = (
   341	            col.where("owner_employee_id", "==", owner_employee_id)
   342	            .where("type", "==", "stagnation")
   343	            .where("payload.task_id", "==", task_id)
   344	            .stream()
   345	        )
   346	        cards: list[Card] = []
   347	        for doc in docs:
   348	            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   349	            if data:
   350	                cards.append(Card.from_dict(data))
   351	        return cards
   352	
   353	    def clear(self) -> None:
   354	        """Clear all stored data across all collections."""
   355	        for col_name in ("agents", "profiles", "messages", "tasks", "schedules", "mail_seeds", "cards"):
   356	            col = self.db.collection(col_name)
   357	            docs = col.stream()
   358	            for doc in docs:
   359	                doc.reference.delete()

   100	    def _add_matching_candidate(self, employee_id: str = "emp_match", agent_id: str = "agent_match") -> None:
   101	        """Register an active agent + public profile that reliably matches the
   102	        deterministic fallback question draft for a task titled
   103	        'Kubernetes upgrade project' (shares 'expertise'/'kubernete'/'upgrade'/
   104	        'project' tokens with FakeConnectionInferencer's >=2 overlap heuristic).
   105	        """
   106	        agent = Agent(
   107	            agent_id=agent_id,
   108	            employee_id=employee_id,
   109	            display_name="Match Employee",
   110	            supported_intents=["connect_ask", "connect_ask_private", "no_connection"],
   111	            active=True,
   112	        )
   113	        profile = Profile(
   114	            employee_id=employee_id,
   115	            name="Match Employee",
   116	            role="SRE",
   117	            items=[
   118	                ProfileItem(
   119	                    key="current_work",
   120	                    body="Has strong expertise in Kubernetes upgrade projects and cluster migrations.",
   121	                    source="job_doc",
   122	                    visibility="public",
   123	                    reviewed=True,
   124	                )
   125	            ],
   126	        )
   127	        self.store.save_agent(agent)
   128	        self.store.save_profile(profile)

   130	    def _make_high_score_task(self, task_id: str = "task_high", owner: str = "emp_owner") -> Task:
   131	        """Task engineered to score far above T2 (score=28 with the pinned weights
   132	        above: capped overdue 10 + capped stale 10 + resched 3*2=6 + untouched 2).
   133	        """
   134	        far_past = _iso(TODAY - timedelta(days=20))
   135	        return Task(
   136	            task_id=task_id,
   137	            owner_employee_id=owner,
   138	            title="Kubernetes upgrade project",
   139	            description="",
   140	            status="todo",
   141	            due_date=far_past,
   142	            created_at=far_past,
   143	            last_updated_at=far_past,
   144	            reschedule_count=3,
   145	            status_changed_at=far_past,
   146	        )
   147	
   148	    def _make_notice_score_task(self, task_id: str = "task_notice", owner: str = "emp_owner") -> Task:
   149	        """Task engineered to land strictly within [T1, T2) (score=4: overdue
   150	        2*1 + resched 1*2; stale=0 since last_updated_at is today, untouched=0
   151	        since status is 'in_progress').
   152	        """
   153	        near_past = _iso(TODAY - timedelta(days=2))
   154	        return Task(
   155	            task_id=task_id,
   156	            owner_employee_id=owner,
   157	            title="Kubernetes upgrade project",
   158	            description="",
   159	            status="in_progress",
   160	            due_date=near_past,
   161	            created_at=near_past,
   162	            last_updated_at=TODAY_STR,
   163	            reschedule_count=1,
   164	            status_changed_at=near_past,
   165	        )

   192	class TestPreviewPublicOnlyIsolation(SecretaryTestBase):
   193	    def test_private_items_do_not_leak_into_preview_candidates(self) -> None:
   194	        # Candidate whose only relevant knowledge is marked private: preview must
   195	        # not surface it even though a full-profile match would succeed, because
   196	        # preview_search() only ever passes public items to Stage-2 inference.
   197	        priv_agent = Agent(
   198	            agent_id="agent_priv",
   199	            employee_id="emp_priv",
   200	            display_name="Private Match Employee",
   201	            supported_intents=["connect_ask", "connect_ask_private", "no_connection"],
   202	            active=True,
   203	        )
   204	        priv_profile = Profile(
   205	            employee_id="emp_priv",
   206	            name="Private Match Employee",
   207	            role="Ops",
   208	            items=[
   209	                ProfileItem(
   210	                    key="public_topic",
   211	                    body="Handles quarterly expense reports.",
   212	                    source="job_doc",
   213	                    visibility="public",
   214	                    reviewed=True,
   215	                ),
   216	                ProfileItem(
   217	                    key="secret_topic",
   218	                    body="Secretly led the Kubernetes upgrade project rollout for leadership only.",
   219	                    source="job_doc",
   220	                    visibility="private",
   221	                    reviewed=True,
   222	                ),
   223	            ],
   224	        )
   225	        self.store.save_agent(priv_agent)
   226	        self.store.save_profile(priv_profile)
   227	
   228	        # Also register a genuine PUBLIC match so the preview is non-empty overall,
   229	        # proving the private-only candidate is excluded specifically (not merely
   230	        # that preview happened to find nothing at all).
   231	        self._add_matching_candidate(employee_id="emp_match", agent_id="agent_match")
   232	
   233	        task = self._make_high_score_task()
   234	        self.store.save_task(task)
   235	
   236	        self.secretary.run_sweep(demo_today=TODAY_STR)
   237	
   238	        card = self.store.find_open_card_for_task("emp_owner", task.task_id)
   239	        self.assertEqual(card.tier, "request_draft")
   240	        candidates = card.payload["preview"]["candidates"]
   241	        candidate_ids = {c["employee_id"] for c in candidates}
   242	
   243	        self.assertIn("emp_match", candidate_ids)
   244	        self.assertNotIn("emp_priv", candidate_ids)
   245	
   246	        for c in candidates:
   247	            self.assertNotIn("secret_topic", c["cited_item_keys"])
   248	            self.assertNotIn("Secretly led the Kubernetes upgrade project rollout", c["reason_text"])

   254	class TestStagnationCardStateMachine(SecretaryTestBase):
   255	    def test_notice_card_promotes_to_request_draft_on_score_increase(self) -> None:
   256	        self._add_matching_candidate()
   257	        task = self._make_notice_score_task()
   258	        self.store.save_task(task)
   259	
   260	        result1 = self.secretary.run_sweep(demo_today=TODAY_STR)
   261	        self.assertEqual(result1["cards_created"], 1)
   262	
   263	        card1 = self.store.find_open_card_for_task("emp_owner", task.task_id)
   264	        self.assertIsNotNone(card1)
   265	        self.assertEqual(card1.tier, "notice")
   266	        self.assertNotIn("question_draft", card1.payload)
   267	        card_id = card1.card_id
   268	
   269	        # Push the task's score well past T2 (heavier reschedule + deeper overdue).
   270	        far_past = _iso(TODAY - timedelta(days=20))
   271	        task.due_date = far_past
   272	        task.last_updated_at = far_past
   273	        task.reschedule_count = 5
   274	        self.store.save_task(task)
   275	
   276	        result2 = self.secretary.run_sweep(demo_today=TODAY_STR)
   277	        self.assertEqual(result2["cards_promoted"], 1)
   278	        self.assertEqual(result2["cards_created"], 0)
   279	
   280	        card2 = self.store.get_card(card_id)
   281	        self.assertEqual(card2.card_id, card_id)  # same card, not a new one
   282	        self.assertEqual(card2.tier, "request_draft")
   283	        self.assertIn("question_draft", card2.payload)
   284	
   285	        all_cards_for_task = self.store.find_cards_for_task("emp_owner", task.task_id)
   286	        self.assertEqual(len(all_cards_for_task), 1)

   288	    def test_task_done_resolves_open_card(self) -> None:
   289	        task = self._make_notice_score_task(task_id="task_done_case")
   290	        self.store.save_task(task)
   291	        self.secretary.run_sweep(demo_today=TODAY_STR)
   292	        card = self.store.find_open_card_for_task("emp_owner", task.task_id)
   293	        self.assertIsNotNone(card)

   296	        self.store.save_task(task)

   298

   300	        resolved_card = self.store.get_card(card.card_id)
   301	        self.assertEqual(resolved_card.status, "resolved")
   302	        self.assertEqual(resolved_card.resolved_reason, "task_done")

   304	    def test_dismissed_task_card_not_recreated(self) -> None:
   305	        task = self._make_notice_score_task(task_id="task_dismiss_case")

   307	        self.secretary.run_sweep(demo_today=TODAY_STR)

   312

   314	        self.secretary.run_sweep(demo_today=TODAY_STR)
   315	        self.assertIsNone(self.store.find_open_card_for_task("emp_owner", task.task_id))
   316	        all_cards = self.store.find_cards_for_task("emp_owner", task.task_id)

   318	        self.assertEqual(all_cards[0].status, "dismissed")

   327	        self.assertEqual(len(cards), 1)

   331	# (d) Confirm CAS: double confirmation dispatches the query only once -------
   332	
   333	
   334	class TestConfirmCardCAS(SecretaryTestBase):
   335	    def test_double_confirm_only_submits_query_once(self) -> None:
   336	        self._add_matching_candidate()
   337	        task = self._make_high_score_task(task_id="task_confirm_case")
   338	        self.store.save_task(task)
   339	        self.secretary.run_sweep(demo_today=TODAY_STR)
   340	
   341	        card = self.store.find_open_card_for_task("emp_owner", task.task_id)
   342	        self.assertEqual(card.tier, "request_draft")
   343	
   344	        first = self.secretary.confirm_stagnation_card(
   345	            card.card_id, edited_question="Edited: need advice on the Kubernetes upgrade."
   346	        )
   347	        self.assertEqual(first["status"], "confirmed")
   348	        self.assertIsNotNone(first["query_audit_id"])
   349	
   350	        messages_after_first = self.store.list_messages()
   351	        query_messages_after_first = [m for m in messages_after_first if m.intent == "query"]
   352	        self.assertEqual(len(query_messages_after_first), 1)
   353	
   354	        second = self.secretary.confirm_stagnation_card(
   355	            card.card_id, edited_question="A different edit that should be ignored."
   356	        )
   357	        self.assertEqual(second["status"], "already_confirmed")
   358	        self.assertEqual(second["query_audit_id"], first["query_audit_id"])
   359	
   360	        messages_after_second = self.store.list_messages()
   361	        self.assertEqual(len(messages_after_second), len(messages_after_first))
   362	        query_messages_after_second = [m for m in messages_after_second if m.intent == "query"]
   363	        self.assertEqual(len(query_messages_after_second), 1)
   364	
   365	        final_card = self.store.get_card(card.card_id)
   366	        self.assertEqual(final_card.status, "confirmed")
   367	        self.assertEqual(final_card.linked_query_audit_id, first["query_audit_id"])

   373	class TestMorningDigestDateRules(SecretaryTestBase):
   374	    def test_schedule_reminders_categorized_by_due_date(self) -> None:
   375	        owner = "emp_digest"
   376	        overdue = Schedule(
   377	            item_id="sch_overdue",
   378	            owner_employee_id=owner,
   379	            kind="expense_deadline",
   380	            title="Overdue expense report",
   381	            due_date=_iso(TODAY - timedelta(days=5)),
   382	        )

   399	            owner_employee_id=owner,
   400	            kind="monthly_report",
   401	            title="Monthly report",
   402	            due_date=_iso(TODAY + timedelta(days=3)),
   403	        )

   406	            self.store.save_schedule(s)
   407	
   408	        digest = self.secretary.get_morning_digest(owner, demo_today=TODAY_STR)
   409	        reminders = digest["reminders"]
   410	
   411	        # No item dropped, duplicated, or misclassified outside the four buckets.
   412	        self.assertEqual(len(reminders), 4)
   413	        by_id = {r["item_id"]: r for r in reminders}
   414	        self.assertEqual(by_id["sch_overdue"]["due_category"], "overdue")
   415	        self.assertEqual(by_id["sch_today"]["due_category"], "today")
   416	        self.assertEqual(by_id["sch_tomorrow"]["due_category"], "tomorrow")
   417	        self.assertEqual(by_id["sch_upcoming"]["due_category"], "upcoming")
   418	
   419	        # Ordering: overdue -> today -> tomorrow -> upcoming.
   420	        self.assertEqual(
   421	            [r["item_id"] for r in reminders],
   422	            ["sch_overdue", "sch_today", "sch_tomorrow", "sch_upcoming"],
   423	        )

   429	class TestProfileDiffApply(SecretaryTestBase):
   430	    def test_apply_profile_diff_adds_item_and_regenerates_both_embeddings(self) -> None:
   431	        owner = "emp_mail"
   432	        profile = Profile(
   433	            employee_id=owner,
   434	            name="Mail Owner",
   435	            role="Engineer",
   436	            items=[

   447	        self.store.save_profile(profile)

   449	        original_embedding_public = list(profile.embedding_public)
   450	
   451	        mail = MailSeed(
   452	            mail_id="mail_1",
   453	            owner_employee_id=owner,
   454	            subject="Project update",
   455	            body="I have been leading the new logistics optimization project this quarter.",
   456	            processed=False,
   457	        )
   458	        self.store.save_mail_seed(mail)
   459	
   460	        self.secretary.run_sweep(demo_today=TODAY_STR)
   461	
   462	        reloaded_mail = self.store.get_mail_seed("mail_1")
   463	        self.assertTrue(reloaded_mail.processed)
   464	
   465	        diff_cards = [
   466	            c for c in self.store.list_cards(owner_employee_id=owner, status="open") if c.type == "profile_diff"

   469	        diff_card = diff_cards[0]
   470	
   471	        result = self.secretary.review_profile_diff(diff_card.card_id, action="apply")
   472	        self.assertEqual(result["status"], "applied")
   473	        self.assertEqual(result["visibility"], "public")
   474	
   475	        updated_profile = self.store.get_profile(owner)
   476	
   477	        # A genuinely NEW item was added (distinct key from the pre-existing one).
   478	        new_item = updated_profile.get_item(result["item_key"])

   482	        self.assertEqual(new_item.source, "mail_seed")
   483	
   484	        # The pre-existing item is untouched.
   485	        background_item = updated_profile.get_item("background")
   486	        self.assertIsNotNone(background_item)
   487	        self.assertEqual(background_item.source, "job_doc")
   488	
   489	        # Both embedding and embedding_public were regenerated (§14.5/§9.3).
   490	        self.assertIsNotNone(updated_profile.embedding)
   491	        self.assertIsNotNone(updated_profile.embedding_public)
   492	        self.assertNotEqual(updated_profile.embedding, original_embedding)
   493	        self.assertNotEqual(updated_profile.embedding_public, original_embedding_public)

   495	        applied_card = self.store.get_card(diff_card.card_id)
   496	        self.assertEqual(applied_card.status, "applied")

   124	def create_app_from_env() -> Any:
   125	    """Production factory: wire FirestoreStore / Gemini adapters from environment.
   126	
   127	    Env vars:
   128	    - USE_FIRESTORE=1        -> FirestoreStore (project from GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT)
   129	    - GEMINI_API_KEY set     -> GeminiEmbedder + GeminiConnectionInferencer
   130	    - DEMO_API_KEY           -> API key protection (create_app default)
   131	
   132	    Run with: uvicorn 'knowledge_discovery.server:create_app_from_env' --factory
   133	    """
   134	    store: Store | None = None
   135	    if os.environ.get("USE_FIRESTORE") == "1":
   136	        from knowledge_discovery.firestore_store import FirestoreStore
   137	
   138	        store = FirestoreStore(project=os.environ.get("GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT"))
   139	
   140	    use_vertex = os.environ.get("GOOGLE_GENAI_USE_VERTEXAI", "").lower() in ("1", "true")
   141	    service: KnowledgeDiscoveryService | None = None
   142	    if os.environ.get("GEMINI_API_KEY") or use_vertex:
   143	        from knowledge_discovery.gemini_adapters import (
   144	            GeminiConnectionInferencer,
   145	            GeminiEmbedder,
   146	        )
   147	
   148	        if store is None:
   149	            store = InMemoryStore()
   150	            from scripts.generate_seeds import populate_store
   151	
   152	            populate_store(store, dry_run=False)
   153	        matching_engine = MatchingEngine(
   154	            embedder=GeminiEmbedder(),
   155	            inferencer=GeminiConnectionInferencer(),
   156	            vector_floor=float(os.environ.get("VECTOR_FLOOR", "0.20")),
   157	            connection_threshold=float(os.environ.get("CONNECTION_THRESHOLD", "0.50")),
   158	            max_dispatch_k=3,
   159	            funnel_limit=20,
   160	        )
   161	        service = KnowledgeDiscoveryService(store=store, matching_engine=matching_engine)
   162	
   163	    return create_app(store=store, service=service)

   215	            raise HTTPException(
   216	                status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
   217	                detail="Invalid or missing API key. Provide header 'X-API-Key' or query parameter 'api_key'.",

   201	
   202	    def __init__(
   203	        self,
   204	        embedder: Embedder | None = None,
   205	        inferencer: ConnectionInferencer | None = None,
   206	        vector_floor: float = 0.20,
   207	        connection_threshold: float = 0.50,
   208	        max_dispatch_k: int = 3,
   209	        funnel_limit: int = 20,
   210	    ) -> None:
   211	        self.embedder = embedder or DeterministicEmbedder()
   212	        self.inferencer = inferencer or FakeConnectionInferencer()
   213	        self.vector_floor = vector_floor
   214	        self.connection_threshold = connection_threshold
   215	        self.max_dispatch_k = max_dispatch_k
   216	        self.funnel_limit = funnel_limit
   217	
   218	    def compute_profile_embedding(self, profile: Profile) -> list[float]:
   219	        """Compute and set embedding and embedding_public for a profile.
   220	
   221	        - embedding: generated from all items (public + private).
   222	        - embedding_public: generated from only public items (preview search §14.4).
   223	        """
   224	        item_text = " ".join(item.body for item in profile.items)
   225	        emb = self.embedder.embed(item_text)
   226	        profile.embedding = emb
   227	
   228	        public_items = [item for item in profile.items if item.visibility == "public"]
   229	        public_item_text = " ".join(item.body for item in public_items)
   230	        emb_pub = self.embedder.embed(public_item_text)
   231	        profile.embedding_public = emb_pub
   232	
   233	        return emb
   234	
   235	    def preview_search(
   236	        self,
   237	        question: str,
   238	        registered_agents: list[Agent],
   239	        profiles: dict[str, Profile],
   240	        max_candidates: int = 3,
   241	        exclude_employee_id: str | None = None,
   242	    ) -> list[PreviewCandidate]:
   243	        """Execute pure, candidate-isolated preview search (public only, no side effects, §14.4).
   244	
   245	        Strict Rules:
   246	        1. 1st stage vector ranking strictly targets embedding_public.
   247	        2. VECTOR_FLOOR is NOT applied to preview.
   248	        3. 2nd stage inference receives ONLY a public-items view of the profile.
   249	        4. Completely pure: zero message dispatch, zero notifications, zero candidate trace.
   250	        """
   251	        q_emb = self.embedder.embed(question)
   252	        active_agents = [
   253	            a for a in registered_agents
   254	            if a.active and (exclude_employee_id is None or a.employee_id != exclude_employee_id)
   255	        ]
   256	
   257	        # 1st stage: rank by embedding_public
   258	        ranked: list[tuple[Agent, Profile, float]] = []
   259	        for agent in active_agents:
   260	            prof = profiles.get(agent.employee_id)
   261	            if prof is None:
   262	                continue
   263	            if prof.embedding_public is None:
   264	                self.compute_profile_embedding(prof)
   265	            sim = self.embedder.similarity(q_emb, prof.embedding_public or [])
   266	            ranked.append((agent, prof, sim))
   267	
   268	        # Sort descending by vector similarity
   269	        ranked.sort(key=lambda x: x[2], reverse=True)
   270	
   271	        candidates: list[PreviewCandidate] = []
   272	        for agent, prof, sim in ranked:
   273	            # Build strictly public-only profile context for isolated Stage-2 inference (C-26/X-1)
   274	            public_items = [
   275	                ProfileItem(
   276	                    key=item.key,
   277	                    body=item.body,
   278	                    source=item.source,
   279	                    visibility="public",
   280	                    reviewed=item.reviewed,
   281	                )
   282	                for item in prof.items
   283	                if item.visibility == "public"
   284	            ]
   285	            public_prof = Profile(
   286	                employee_id=prof.employee_id,
   287	                name=prof.name,
   288	                role=prof.role,
   289	                items=public_items,
   290	                embedding=prof.embedding_public,
   291	                embedding_public=prof.embedding_public,
   292	            )
   293	
   294	            res = self.inferencer.infer_connection(question, public_prof)
   295	            if res.connection is not None and res.connection.score >= self.connection_threshold:
   296	                # Cited item keys must only come from public items
   297	                safe_cited = [k for k in res.cited_item_keys if public_prof.get_item(k) is not None]
   298	                candidates.append(
   299	                    PreviewCandidate(
   300	                        employee_id=prof.employee_id,
   301	                        name=prof.name,
   302	                        reason_text=res.connection.reason_text,
   303	                        cited_item_keys=safe_cited,
   304	                        score=res.connection.score,
   305	                    )
   306	                )
   307	                if len(candidates) >= max_candidates:
   308	                    break
   309	
   310	        return candidates
   311	
   312	    def screen_funnel(self, question: str, all_profiles: list[Profile]) -> list[FunnelCandidate]:
   313	        """Track 1: Screen funnel ranking top 20 across all profiles for scale display (C-16)."""
   314	        q_emb = self.embedder.embed(question)
   315	        scored: list[tuple[Profile, float]] = []
   316	
   317	        for prof in all_profiles:
   318	            if prof.embedding is None:
   319	                self.compute_profile_embedding(prof)
   320	            sim = self.embedder.similarity(q_emb, prof.embedding or [])
   321	            scored.append((prof, sim))
   322	
   323	        # Sort by similarity descending
   324	        scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
   325	
   326	        top_profiles = scored[: self.funnel_limit]
   327	        return [
   328	            FunnelCandidate(
   329	                employee_id=p.employee_id,

+    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
+    # Task operations (tasks collection)
+    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+    def save_task(self, task: Task) -> None:
+        """Save or update a task record."""
+        doc_ref = self.db.collection("tasks").document(task.task_id)
+        doc_ref.set(task.to_dict())
+
+    def get_task(self, task_id: str) -> Task | None:
+        """Retrieve a task by task_id."""
+        doc = self.db.collection("tasks").document(task_id).get()
+        if hasattr(doc, "exists") and not doc.exists:
+            return None
+        data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
+        if not data:
+            return None
+        return Task.from_dict(data)
+
+    def list_tasks(self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None) -> list[Task]:
+        """List tasks, optionally filtered by owner_employee_id."""
+        col = self.db.collection("tasks")
+        if owner_employee_id is not None:
+            docs = col.where("owner_employee_id", "==", owner_employee_id).stream()
+        else:
+            docs = col.stream()
+        tasks: list[Task] = []
+        for doc in docs:
+            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
+            if data:
+                tasks.append(Task.from_dict(data))
+        return tasks
+
+    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
+    # Schedule operations (schedules collection)
+    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+    def save_schedule(self, schedule: Schedule) -> None:
+        """Save or update a schedule reminder."""
+        doc_ref = self.db.collection("schedules").document(schedule.item_id)
+        doc_ref.set(schedule.to_dict())
+
+    def get_schedule(self, item_id: str) -> Schedule | None:

+        if hasattr(doc, "exists") and not doc.exists:
+            return None
+        data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None

+            return None
+        return Schedule.from_dict(data)

+    def list_schedules(self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None) -> list[Schedule]:
+        """List schedules, optionally filtered by owner_employee_id."""

+            docs = col.where("owner_employee_id", "==", owner_employee_id).stream()
+        else:
+            docs = col.stream()
+        schedules: list[Schedule] = []

+    def get_mail_seed(self, mail_id: str) -> MailSeed | None:

+    def list_mail_seeds(
+        self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None, unprocessed_only: bool = False
+    ) -> list[MailSeed]:
+        """List mail seeds, optionally filtered by owner and/or unprocessed status."""
+        col = self.db.collection("mail_seeds")
+        query = col
+        if owner_employee_id is not None:
+            query = query.where("owner_employee_id", "==", owner_employee_id)
+        if unprocessed_only:
+            query = query.where("processed", "==", False)
+        docs = query.stream()
+        mails: list[MailSeed] = []
+        for doc in docs:
+            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
+            if data:
+                mails.append(MailSeed.from_dict(data))
+        return mails

+    def list_cards(
+        self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None, status: str | None = None
+    ) -> list[Card]:
+        """List cards, optionally filtered by owner_employee_id and/or status."""
+        col = self.db.collection("cards")
+        query = col
+        if owner_employee_id is not None:
+            query = query.where("owner_employee_id", "==", owner_employee_id)
+        if status is not None:
+            query = query.where("status", "==", status)
+        docs = query.stream()
+        cards: list[Card] = []
+        for doc in docs:
+            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
+            if data:
+                cards.append(Card.from_dict(data))

+    def find_open_card_for_task(self, owner_employee_id: str, task_id: str) -> Card | None:
+        """Find an open stagnation card for the given task and owner."""
+        col = self.db.collection("cards")
+        docs = (
+            col.where("owner_employee_id", "==", owner_employee_id)
+            .where("type", "==", "stagnation")
+            .where("status", "==", "open")
+            .where("payload.task_id", "==", task_id)
+            .limit(1)
+            .stream()
+        )
+        for doc in docs:
+            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
+            if data:
+                return Card.from_dict(data)
+        return None
+
+    def find_cards_for_task(self, owner_employee_id: str, task_id: str) -> list[Card]:
+        """Find all stagnation cards for the given task and owner."""
+        col = self.db.collection("cards")
+        docs = (
+            col.where("owner_employee_id", "==", owner_employee_id)
+            .where("type", "==", "stagnation")
+            .where("payload.task_id", "==", task_id)
+            .stream()
+        )
+        cards: list[Card] = []
+        for doc in docs:
+            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
+            if data:
+                cards.append(Card.from_dict(data))
+        return cards

+    def get_public_text(self) -> str:
+        """Combine only public profile items into text for public embedding."""
+        parts = [f"{self.name} - {self.role}"]
+        for item in self.items:
+            if item.visibility == "public":

+@dataclass
+class Task:
+    """Task entity for secretary stagnation detection (§14.2).
+
+    Attributes:
+        task_id: Unique task identifier.
+        owner_employee_id: Identifier of the employee owning this task.
+        title: Task title.
+        description: Detailed task description.
+        status: 'todo' | 'in_progress' | 'done'.
+        due_date: Due date in ISO format (YYYY-MM-DD).
+        created_at: ISO8601 creation timestamp.
+        last_updated_at: ISO8601 last updated timestamp.
+        reschedule_count: Number of times this task has been rescheduled.
+        status_changed_at: ISO8601 timestamp of last status change.
+    """
+
+    task_id: str
+    owner_employee_id: str
+    title: str
+    description: str = ""
+    status: str = "todo"  # "todo" | "in_progress" | "done"
+    due_date: str = ""
+    created_at: str = field(default_factory=utc_now_iso)
+    last_updated_at: str = field(default_factory=utc_now_iso)
+    reschedule_count: int = 0
+    status_changed_at: str = field(default_factory=utc_now_iso)
+
+    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        return {
+            "task_id": self.task_id,
+            "owner_employee_id": self.owner_employee_id,
+            "title": self.title,
+            "description": self.description,
+            "status": self.status,
+            "due_date": self.due_date,
+            "created_at": self.created_at,
+            "last_updated_at": self.last_updated_at,
+            "reschedule_count": self.reschedule_count,
+            "status_changed_at": self.status_changed_at,
+        }
+
+    @classmethod
+    def from_dict(cls, data: dict[str, Any]) -> Task:
+        return cls(
+            task_id=data["task_id"],
+            owner_employee_id=data["owner_employee_id"],
+            title=data.get("title", ""),
+            description=data.get("description", ""),
+            status=data.get("status", "todo"),
+            due_date=data.get("due_date", ""),
+            created_at=data.get("created_at", utc_now_iso()),
+            last_updated_at=data.get("last_updated_at", utc_now_iso()),
+            reschedule_count=int(data.get("reschedule_count", 0)),
+            status_changed_at=data.get("status_changed_at", utc_now_iso()),
+        )

+@dataclass
+class Card:
+    """Secretary card for stagnation alerts or profile diff proposals (§14.2).
+
+    Attributes:
+        card_id: Unique card identifier.
+        owner_employee_id: Identifier of the card recipient employee.
+        type: 'stagnation' | 'profile_diff'.
+        tier: 'notice' | 'request_draft' | None.
+        payload: Card payload (stagnation details or profile diff proposal).
+        status: 'open' | 'confirmed' | 'dismissed' | 'applied' | 'resolved'.
+        resolved_reason: 'task_done' | 'score_below_t1' | None.
+        linked_query_audit_id: Linked query audit_id if confirmed.
+        created_at: ISO8601 timestamp.
+        updated_at: ISO8601 timestamp.
+    """
+
+    card_id: str
+    owner_employee_id: str
+    type: str  # "stagnation" | "profile_diff"
+    tier: str | None = None  # "notice" | "request_draft" | None
+    payload: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
+    status: str = "open"  # "open" | "confirmed" | "dismissed" | "applied" | "resolved"
+    resolved_reason: str | None = None  # "task_done" | "score_below_t1" | None
+    linked_query_audit_id: str | None = None
+    created_at: str = field(default_factory=utc_now_iso)
+    updated_at: str = field(default_factory=utc_now_iso)
+
+    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        return {
+            "card_id": self.card_id,
+            "owner_employee_id": self.owner_employee_id,
+            "type": self.type,
+            "tier": self.tier,
+            "payload": dict(self.payload),
+            "status": self.status,
+            "resolved_reason": self.resolved_reason,
+            "linked_query_audit_id": self.linked_query_audit_id,
+            "created_at": self.created_at,
+            "updated_at": self.updated_at,
+        }
+
+    @classmethod
+    def from_dict(cls, data: dict[str, Any]) -> Card:
+        return cls(
+            card_id=data["card_id"],
+            owner_employee_id=data["owner_employee_id"],
+            type=data.get("type", "stagnation"),
+            tier=data.get("tier"),
+            payload=dict(data.get("payload", {})),
+            status=data.get("status", "open"),
+            resolved_reason=data.get("resolved_reason"),
+            linked_query_audit_id=data.get("linked_query_audit_id"),
+            created_at=data.get("created_at", utc_now_iso()),
+            updated_at=data.get("updated_at", utc_now_iso()),
+        )

+    # Task operations
+    def save_task(self, task: Task) -> None:
+        stored = deepcopy(task)
+        self._tasks[stored.task_id] = stored
+
+    def get_task(self, task_id: str) -> Task | None:
+        task = self._tasks.get(task_id)
+        return deepcopy(task) if task is not None else None
+
+    def list_tasks(self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None) -> list[Task]:
+        tasks = list(self._tasks.values())
+        if owner_employee_id is not None:
+            tasks = [t for t in tasks if t.owner_employee_id == owner_employee_id]
+        return [deepcopy(t) for t in tasks]
+
+    # Schedule operations
+    def save_schedule(self, schedule: Schedule) -> None:
+        stored = deepcopy(schedule)
+        self._schedules[stored.item_id] = stored
+
+    def get_schedule(self, item_id: str) -> Schedule | None:
+        s = self._schedules.get(item_id)
+        return deepcopy(s) if s is not None else None
+
+    def list_schedules(self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None) -> list[Schedule]:
+        schedules = list(self._schedules.values())
+        if owner_employee_id is not None:
+            schedules = [s for s in schedules if s.owner_employee_id == owner_employee_id]
+        return [deepcopy(s) for s in schedules]
+
+    # MailSeed operations
+    def save_mail_seed(self, mail: MailSeed) -> None:
+        stored = deepcopy(mail)
+        self._mail_seeds[stored.mail_id] = stored
+
+    def get_mail_seed(self, mail_id: str) -> MailSeed | None:
+        m = self._mail_seeds.get(mail_id)
+        return deepcopy(m) if m is not None else None
+
+    def list_mail_seeds(
+        self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None, unprocessed_only: bool = False
+    ) -> list[MailSeed]:
+        mails = list(self._mail_seeds.values())
+        if owner_employee_id is not None:
+            mails = [m for m in mails if m.owner_employee_id == owner_employee_id]
+        if unprocessed_only:
+            mails = [m for m in mails if not m.processed]
+        return [deepcopy(m) for m in mails]
+
+    # Card operations
+    def save_card(self, card: Card) -> None:
+        stored = deepcopy(card)
+        self._cards[stored.card_id] = stored
+
+    def get_card(self, card_id: str) -> Card | None:
+        c = self._cards.get(card_id)
+        return deepcopy(c) if c is not None else None
+
+    def list_cards(
+        self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None, status: str | None = None
+    ) -> list[Card]:
+        cards = list(self._cards.values())
+        if owner_employee_id is not None:
+            cards = [c for c in cards if c.owner_employee_id == owner_employee_id]
+        if status is not None:
+            cards = [c for c in cards if c.status == status]
+        return [deepcopy(c) for c in cards]
+
+    def find_open_card_for_task(self, owner_employee_id: str, task_id: str) -> Card | None:
+        for c in self._cards.values():
+            if (
+                c.owner_employee_id == owner_employee_id
+                and c.type == "stagnation"
+                and c.status == "open"
+                and c.payload.get("task_id") == task_id
+            ):
+                return deepcopy(c)
+        return None
+
+    def find_cards_for_task(self, owner_employee_id: str, task_id: str) -> list[Card]:
+        results: list[Card] = []
+        for c in self._cards.values():
+            if (
+                c.owner_employee_id == owner_employee_id
+                and c.type == "stagnation"
+                and c.payload.get("task_id") == task_id
+            ):
+                results.append(deepcopy(c))
+        return results

2026-08-19T04:20:09.535500Z ERROR codex_models_manager::manager: failed to renew cache TTL: missing field `base_instructions` at line 95 column 5
codex
反論あり。新規欠陥は5件です。

### E-6 — 帯変化のない再sweepでもプレビューを再実行する

- 種別: 実装
- 重大度: mid
- 該当: [secretary.py:408–517](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/secretary.py:408)、[test_secretary.py:320](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/tests/test_secretary.py:320)
- なぜ§14の範囲を超えるか: §14.2は同じ帯の`open/*`を「score・evidence_lineのみ更新」としているが、既存`request_draft`でも質問下書きと候補推論を再生成し、`preview_search`監査行まで追加している。実測でも2回目のsweepで監査行が重複した。定期実行のたびに最大4体分の推論・監査書込み・候補変動が発生する、未指定の継続再探索機能になっている。

### E-7 — 実行時に到達しないLLM抽象化を二系統保持している

- 種別: 実装
- 重大度: mid
- 該当: [server.py:435](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/server.py:435)、[secretary.py:194](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/secretary.py:194)、[secretary.py:224](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/secretary.py:224)、[secretary.py:283](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/secretary.py:283)
- なぜ§14の範囲を超えるか: 本番の唯一の`SecretaryService`生成箇所は`llm_client`を渡さず、テストも同様なので、Geminiプロンプト・JSON解析・`GEMINI_MODEL`設定は全て未使用で、常にテンプレート／ヒューリスティック側へ落ちる。§14に必要な実行経路ではなく、未配線の将来経路とフォールバックを約70行維持する形になっており、凍結前の保守負債になる。

### E-8 — dismissを全カード・全状態に適用できる汎用操作へ広げている

- 種別: 設計
- 重大度: mid
- 該当: [server.py:492](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/server.py:492)、[secretary.py:773](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/secretary.py:773)、[requester.html:289](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/web/requester.html:289)
- なぜ§14の範囲を超えるか: UIでの利用はopenな停滞カードだけで、profile diffには既に専用の「見送り」経路がある。一方、汎用エンドポイントはカード種別・現在状態を検証せず、`confirmed`・`applied`・`resolved`やprofile diffまで`dismissed`へ変更できる。§14の個別状態機械にはない万能遷移を追加している。

### E-9 — UIもSchedulerも使わない外部APIパラメータを公開している

- 種別: 設計
- 重大度: low
- 該当: [server.py:104](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/server.py:104)、[server.py:113](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/server.py:113)、[server.py:445](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/server.py:445)、[server.py:454](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/server.py:454)
- なぜ§14の範囲を超えるか: `DEMO_TODAY`は環境変数として定義済みなのに、sweepでは本文とqueryの二経路、digestでもqueryから日付を上書きできる。さらにprofile diff APIはUIが送らない任意`item_key`変更を受け付ける。§14.5/14.7にない外部設定面を増やし、テスト用ノブを本番契約へ漏らしている。

### E-10 — 呼出しのないCRUD・public-text抽象化が残っている

- 種別: 実装
- 重大度: low
- 該当: [models.py:152](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/models.py:152)、[store.py:92](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/store.py:92)、[store.py:108](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/store.py:108)、[firestore_store.py:189](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/firestore_store.py:189)
- なぜ§14の範囲を超えるか: `get_public_text()`、`get_task()`、`get_schedule()`には呼出し箇所がない。特にpublic embeddingは別の文字列組立てを直接実装しており、未使用ヘルパーとは内容も一致しない。4名デモの必要操作を超えて、ABC・InMemory・Firestoreの三層に対称CRUDを先回り実装している。

検証では全74テストが成功しましたが、E-6は既存テストが「カードが1枚か」だけを確認しているため検出されません。
tokens used
154,240
反論あり。新規欠陥は5件です。

### E-6 — 帯変化のない再sweepでもプレビューを再実行する

- 種別: 実装
- 重大度: mid
- 該当: [secretary.py:408–517](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/secretary.py:408)、[test_secretary.py:320](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/tests/test_secretary.py:320)
- なぜ§14の範囲を超えるか: §14.2は同じ帯の`open/*`を「score・evidence_lineのみ更新」としているが、既存`request_draft`でも質問下書きと候補推論を再生成し、`preview_search`監査行まで追加している。実測でも2回目のsweepで監査行が重複した。定期実行のたびに最大4体分の推論・監査書込み・候補変動が発生する、未指定の継続再探索機能になっている。

### E-7 — 実行時に到達しないLLM抽象化を二系統保持している

- 種別: 実装
- 重大度: mid
- 該当: [server.py:435](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/server.py:435)、[secretary.py:194](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/secretary.py:194)、[secretary.py:224](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/secretary.py:224)、[secretary.py:283](/Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery/src/knowledge_discovery/secretary.py:283)
- なぜ§14の範囲を超えるか: 本番の唯一の`SecretaryService`生成箇所は`llm_client`を渡さず、テストも同様なので、Geminiプロンプト・JSON解析・`GEMINI_MODEL`設定は全て未使用で、常にテンプレート／ヒューリスティック側へ落ちる。§14に必要な実行経路ではなく、未配線の将来経路とフォールバックを約70行維持する形になっており、凍結前の保守負債になる。
